诺沃斯克-17　封城第十个月

凛冬铁轨

锅炉房的气窗还开着。阿列克谢必须趁夜出门，给 602 室的母亲找药，也要查清编号 0420 的妹妹被送去了哪里。

剧情分镜

封城第十个月序章 / 602 室与货运调度室 / 开场

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、卡佳、拉娜、清杀队

  1. Z 年，凛冬。西伯利亚铁路枢纽诺沃斯克-17，封城的第十个月。 一种叫「灰鳞病」的高烧在城里蔓延。发病的人被叫作「狂暴者」，装进车皮拉走，再也没有回来。

    English

    The Year of Z, deep winter. Novorsk-17, a Siberian rail hub, ten months under quarantine. A fever called Grey Scale spreads through the town. The sick are called Rabid, loaded into freight cars, and never come back.

  2. 你是阿列克谢·莫罗佐夫，货运调度员。602 室里，母亲卡佳靠腹膜透析活着。 透析液由区配给委员会发放——每一袋，都要拿「听话」去换。

    English

    You are Aleksei Morozov, a freight dispatcher. In Room 602 your mother Katya lives on peritoneal dialysis. The fluid is issued by the District Rationing Committee. Every bag is paid for with obedience.

  3. 十天前，你在 004 号车皮的入编调拨单上签了字。随车联铆在门框上。这节闷罐送完一批就空车回场。 第二天，母亲领到了下一批腹透液。

    English

    Ten days ago you signed Car 004 into permanent rotation. The signed copy was riveted to the door frame. The car would return empty after each run. The next morning Mother received her next batch of fluid.

  4. 筛查那天，妹妹拉娜被带走了。留给你的只有一张红码转运回执。 档案编号 0420，去向栏被黑章整个盖住了。

    English

    Lana was taken at screening. They left you a red-code transfer receipt. File 0420. A black stamp covers the destination field.

  5. 卡佳：阿廖沙……拉娜还没回电报吗？ 你把转运单折好，塞进大衣内袋。 阿列克谢：妈，铁路医院派她去外地支援医疗了。等忙完这阵，她就回来。

    English

    Katya: Alyosha... still no telegram from Lana? You fold the transfer notice and slip it into your inside coat pocket. Aleksei: Mama, the railway hospital sent her out of town to help a medical team. She'll be home when the work is done.

  6. 清杀队：封楼！所有人退回室内，天亮之前不许出门！ 焊枪封死了单元门。白天你替台账搬运物资；夜里，整栋楼只剩探照灯还醒着。

    English

    OSK: Seal the building! Everyone inside. Nobody leaves before dawn! The welders close the stairwell door. By day you haul for the ledger; by night only the searchlights are awake.

  7. 只有锅炉房那扇锈死的通风口，还通向外面。每一夜只够去一个地方，天亮之前必须回来。 你在心里对自己说：先把药带回家，再找到拉娜。

    English

    Only the rusted vent of the boiler room still leads outside. One place per night, and back before dawn. You tell yourself: bring the medicine home first, then find Lana.

和母亲说话据点 / 602 室 / 602 室

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、卡佳

  1. 卡佳：阿廖沙，手怎么这么凉？把手套放到炉边吧。 阿列克谢：我没事，妈。今晚我会把背包里的东西理好。

    English

    Katya: Alyosha, your hands are freezing. Leave your gloves by the stove. Aleksei: I'm all right, Mama. I'll sort what I brought back tonight.

  2. 卡佳：别总想着替所有人扛事。先坐下喝口热水，让我看看你有没有伤着。 她说得很轻，像怕惊动透析机。

    English

    Katya: You can't carry everyone. Sit down and drink something warm; let me see if you're hurt. She speaks softly, as if not to wake the dialysis machine.

母亲在等据点 / 602 室 / 602 室

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、卡佳

  1. 卡佳：机器还能撑一阵。别为了我一个人，把自己也搭进去。 阿列克谢看了看透析机旁空着的位置，没有回答。

    English

    Katya: The machine can hold for a little while. Don't lose yourself trying to save me. Aleksei looks at the empty space beside the dialysis machine and says nothing.

母亲的遗物据点 / 602 室 / 602 室

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

  1. 屋里只剩透析机停下后的静默。你把母亲留下的围巾叠好，放在床边。

    English

    Only silence remains after the dialysis machine stops. You fold your mother's scarf and set it beside the bed.

交付透析液据点 / 602 室 / 602 室

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、卡佳

  1. 阿列克谢把一袋透析液放到机器旁。 卡佳：这一袋先留着，明早再换。你总算肯让我也替你省着点力气了。

    English

    Aleksei sets a bag of dialysis fluid beside the machine. Katya: Keep this one for the morning. At last you're letting me help you save a little strength.

维京娜的线索据点 / 602 室 / 602 室

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、维京娜

  1. 楼道里，邻居维京娜隔着半掩的门和人说话。她正抱怨电报局把一份旧底单退回了档案馆。

    English

    In the hall, neighbor Vikina talks through a half-open door. She complains that the telegraph office returned an old dispatch copy to the archive.

  2. 维京娜：退件封面写着 0420。我在电报局窗口瞥见的，底单已经退回档案馆。谁管那份旧名单？我只想找回配给单。 她没意识到自己说出了什么。

    English

    Vikina: The cover said 0420. I saw it at the telegraph window. The copy went back to the archive. Who cares about an old list? I just want my ration slip. She doesn’t realize what she has just said.

  3. 阿列克谢：0420……拉娜的档案号。 名单被退回档案馆，底单就在电报局。今晚我去找它。

    English

    Aleksei: 0420... Lana's file number. The list went back to the archive; the dispatch copy is at the telegraph office. I'll find it tonight.

    选项与回执
    1. 追问维京娜，确认底单去向
      English

      Ask Vikina where the copy went

      维京娜的笑意淡了下去。她只确认底单已经退回档案馆，随后把门轻轻合上。阿列克谢没有再问，把这句话记进日志。

      English

      Vikina's smile fades. She confirms only that the copy went back to the archive, then gently closes the door. Aleksei writes it in his log and asks nothing more.

    2. 记下线索，暂不惊动她
      English

      Keep the clue to yourself

      阿列克谢没有敲门。他把「0420 · 退档」记在手套内侧。维京娜仍不知道自己递出了一条线索。

      English

      Aleksei does not knock. He writes “0420 · returned to archive” inside his glove. Vikina never learns she gave him the lead.

账面死人第一幕 / 城北四号铁路冷库 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、切尔诺夫、谢尔盖、玛丽亚（遗体）、灰烬

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 钢笔尖刺破纸背：谢尔盖、玛丽亚。 电传机判了他们「零」。谢尔盖还在呼吸；玛丽亚昨夜冻死了，她的名字也不能被抹掉。

    English

    The nib bites through the page: Sergey. Maria. The teletype counted them as zero. Sergey is still breathing; Maria froze to death last night, and her name must not be erased.

  2. 柱子后面，谢尔盖不肯放下女儿。你扶住玛丽亚冻僵的膝弯，和他一起把她抱出冷库。

    English

    Behind the column, Sergey refuses to put his daughter down. You support Maria's stiffened knees and help him carry her out of the cold store.

  3. 那只脖子上嵌着断铁丝的灰狼犬无声地走上前，把冻湿的前爪按在你的靴面上。 你叫它「灰烬」。

    English

    The grey wolfdog with the snapped wire collar walks up without a sound and plants a wet paw on your boot. You call him Ash.

账面死人第一幕 / 城北四号铁路冷库 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 2

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、切尔诺夫、谢尔盖、玛丽亚（遗体）、灰烬

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 你低下头，数清了公家的箱子。谢尔盖仍跪在三号柱后，怀里的玛丽亚没有呼吸。

    English

    You lower your head and count the Committee's crates. Sergey remains behind column three, kneeling with Maria in his arms. She is not breathing.

  2. 你没有回头。身后，配给箱的电子锁又响了一声；谢尔盖还抱着女儿，没人来替他们开门。

    English

    You do not look back. The ration crate's lock snaps shut again; Sergey still holds his daughter, and no one comes to open the door for them.

  3. 收发亭旁，那只灰狼犬仍缩在风雪里。你没有剪断锈铁丝，也没有把黑麦饼递过去；它留在原地，颈上的旧伤仍清晰可见。

    English

    The wolfdog remains by the dispatch hut in the snow. You did not cut the wire or offer the bread; it stays where it is, its old neck scar still visible.

铁壳号编组单第一幕 / 夜间广轨货场 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、扎哈尔

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 风把油墨吹开了一角。你用拇指压住 0420，指甲下染上一点蓝。

    English

    The wind lifts a corner of the ink. You press a thumb over 0420, and a little blue stains the nail.

  2. 探照灯从侧线上慢慢扫过。你贴着煤水塔的阴影绕开守卫，等雪盖住脚印才离开。

    English

    A searchlight crawls across the siding. You skirt the guards in the coal tower's shadow and leave after the snow softens your tracks.

  3. 你把那张纸贴在胸口。回到 602 时，母亲的围巾还搭在床沿；你隔着大衣摸了摸那串号码，没有把它说出口。

    English

    You press the paper to your chest. Back in Room 602, Mother's scarf still hangs at the bedside. You touch the number through your coat and keep it to yourself.

退烧的母亲第一幕 / 夜间广轨货场 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、退烧的母亲、清杀队

战役货场首次靠近锅炉房通风口 查看地图

  1. 心声：手背上有灰鳞。往暗处退。……等等，她在叫谁开门？

    English

    Inner voice: Gray scales on her hand. Back into shadow. …Wait. Who is she asking to open the door?

  2. 她隔着铁栅栏向楼里喊：「科利亚……给妈开条缝。妈退烧了，你摸摸。妈不进去，只看一眼小安东。」

    English

    She calls through the bars: "Kolya... open the door a little. Mama's fever broke; feel my forehead. I won't come in. I only want to see little Anton."

  3. 探照灯照亮栅栏，清杀队从拐角出来。 女人：「别开枪……我退烧了。」

    English

    A searchlight catches the bars as the purge squad rounds the corner. Woman: “Don't shoot… my fever broke.”

  4. 两声闷响。她倒在门边，再没动过。清杀队走远了，铜钥匙落在沟沿。

    English

    Two muffled shots. She falls by the gate and never moves again. The squad walks away. Her brass keys lie by the gutter.

  5. 告示上说，高热后的感染者只会恶化，最后失去神志。可她还认得儿子，烧也退了，手背的鳞正一片片脱落。阿列克谢怔在煤堆边：并不是人人都能熬过来，可眼前这个女人正在好转。

    English

    The notice says the infected only worsen after a high fever, until they lose their minds. But she still knows her son. Her fever has broken, and scales peel from her hand. Aleksei stares from the coal pile: not everyone makes it through, but this woman is getting better.

东街食堂第二幕 / 东街工人食堂 · 后厨 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、瓦西里、费奥多尔

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 刀刃卡进骨缝的那一瞬，瓦西里眼里的紫黑褪去了。 他只说了一个词：「……Пить。」——渴。

    English

    As the blade jams, the black-purple drains from Vasily's eyes. He says one word: "...Пить." Thirsty.

  2. 两天后的深夜，你发着高烧回到东街食堂，跪在费奥多尔面前： 你选了刀，只是因为恐惧让它最快。

    English

    Two nights later, feverish, you go back to the East Street canteen and kneel before Fyodor: you chose the cleaver only because fear made it quickest.

  3. 老人没有抬手。他把儿子的旧袄披在你肩上： 「把他活着时候的名字，写进你的本子里。」

    English

    The old man does not raise a hand. He drapes his son's coat over your shoulders: "Write the name he had when he was alive. Put it in your book."

回退手稿第二幕 / 旧传染病疗养院 · 地下室 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、梁诺夫、古丽娅、安娜

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 撬棍崩开了生锈的笼锁。你替她解开嵌进肉里的皮带—— 那是她怕自己伤人，亲手勒上去的。

    English

    The crowbar breaks the rusted lock. You undo the straps sunk into her wrists. She had tied them herself, afraid of what she might do.

  2. 「昨晚装车前，三十六度八。我看着她把最后半片阿司匹林塞过网眼。她说，哥会来接我的。」

    English

    Before they loaded her last night, it was 36.8. I watched her pass her last half aspirin through the wire. She said her brother would come.

  3. 安娜（0419）与古丽娅医师搀扶着跟上了你。 笼门的轴在极夜的风雪里，发出一声长长的、如释重负的悲鸣。

    English

    Anna (0419) and Dr. Gulya lean on each other and follow you out. In the polar wind the cage hinge gives one long, relieved scream.

食堂卡刀第二幕 / 东街工人食堂 · 后厨 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、瓦西里、费奥多尔

瓦西里扑出后的近战输入 查看地图

  1. 刀刃卡在锁骨里。瓦西里还压着你，费奥多尔在一旁伸手。把刀拔出来！

    English

    The blade is caught in his collarbone. Vasily is still on top of you; Fyodor reaches from beside you. Pull the blade free!

费奥多尔的回访第二幕 / 东街工人食堂 · 后厨 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、瓦西里、费奥多尔

瓦西里死亡并完成相应夜潜后 查看地图

  1. 瓦西里的眼睛清亮了一瞬。他喉咙里挤出一个词：「……Пить。」——水，渴。

    English

    Vasily's eyes clear for one instant. He forces out a word: "...Пить." Water. Thirst.

  2. 两天后的深夜，你发着高烧回到食堂，在费奥多尔面前跪下。老人没有举起手，只摸了摸儿子渐渐冷下来的脸。

    English

    Two nights later, feverish, you return to the canteen and kneel before Fyodor. He raises no hand; he only touches his son's cooling face.

  3. 费奥多尔把瓦西里的旧坎肩披在你肩上：「别把他当成怪物。把他活着时候的名字写下来。」

    English

    Fyodor lays Vasily's old vest over your shoulders. "Do not call him a monster. Write down the name he had while he was alive."

  4. 灶台后响了两下钉锤。帮着钉瓦西里的木箱的老人拎起帆布包：「我叫帕维尔，在机务段修了一辈子车门。下回碰上焊死的门，叫上我。你得腾出手来接人。」

    English

    Two hammer taps behind the stove. The old man helping with Vasily’s coffin picks up his canvas bag. “Pavel. I fixed railcar doors my whole life. Next time you find one welded shut, call me. You’ll need your hands free to catch people.”

失母后的黑市暂住第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 602 室

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

查看地图

  1. 废信号室的短波机还活着。阿列克谢把门栓扣上，在炉边留下一盏遮光灯。这里不安全，却能让他在黑市搜捕间隙喘口气。

    English

    The shortwave set in the abandoned signal room still works. Aleksei bolts the door and shades a lamp by the stove. It is not safe, but it gives him a place to breathe between market searches.

  2. 这不是家。他还会回 602 看一眼母亲留下的东西，再沿着维京娜无心说出的线索去找拉娜。

    English

    This is not home. He will return to 602 for what Mother left behind, then follow Vikina's offhand clue to Lana.

黑市与老轨第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、药贩、伊万

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 伊万冷冷地收下废钢，用粗黑的指甲在油纸上划出一道线： 「顺着西站水塔底下的排污检修沟摸过去。」

    English

    Ivan takes the scrap without warmth and drags a black thumbnail across the oil paper: "Follow the maintenance trench under the West Yard water tower."

  2. 「记住——第三根冻裂的枕木底下埋着雷。不想死，就别往上踩。」

    English

    "And remember: there's a mine under the third cracked sleeper. Step on it if you want to die."

  3. 一笔公平的买卖，谁也不欠谁。 松林庄园的方向，在油纸上亮了起来。

    English

    A fair trade. Nobody owes anybody. On the oil paper, the way to Pine Manor lights up.

黑市与老轨第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 抉择余波表 / 选项 2

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、药贩、伊万

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 你把救命的抗生素，塞进那些冻烂了脚趾的苦人手里。 没有人道谢——他们只是不敢相信。

    English

    You press your antibiotics into the hands of people whose toes have gone black with frost. Nobody thanks you. They simply cannot believe it.

  2. 「老轨」伊万浑浊的眼底狠狠颤了一下。他没有再提废钢的事。

    English

    Something shakes loose behind Old Ivan's clouded eyes. He does not mention the scrap again.

  3. 他把一只烤热了的厚帆布手套塞进你怀里，一言不发，跟上了你的脚步。

    English

    He shoves a warmed canvas glove against your chest and, without a word, falls in behind you.

黑市与老轨第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 抉择余波表 / 选项 3

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、药贩、伊万

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 他替你引开了哨兵。 而你，从外面落下了铁门的锁。

    English

    He drew the sentries off for you. And you dropped the bolt on the iron door from the outside.

  2. 门后先是一声难以置信的闷哼，紧接着是微声步枪的点射——短促得像踩碎了冰碴。

    English

    Behind the door: one grunt of disbelief, then a suppressed burst, as short as ice breaking underfoot.

  3. 背包被抢来的物资撑得满满当当。 《工作日志》的背面，多了一道洗不掉的划痕。

    English

    Your pack is stuffed with what you took. On the back page of the logbook there is one more stroke that will not wash out.

4.17 MHz 与旧信号检修室第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、叶莲娜

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 短波信号的尽头，金发的叶莲娜关掉发射机。厚实、剪裁合身的冬衣让她看起来比棚外的人体面；她摘下耳机，主动把频率表递给你。

    English

    At the end of the shortwave signal, blonde Yelena switches off the transmitter. Her well-cut winter coat is better kept than anything outside; she removes her headphones and hands you the frequency sheet.

  2. 你选择带她同行。叶莲娜背起监听设备，交出进入松林庄园的排污路线；她也能用无线电命令一名 OSK 暂停行动。

    English

    You invite her along. Yelena shoulders her listening gear and shares the drainage route into Pine Manor; her radio can halt one OSK patrol briefly.

4.17 MHz 与旧信号检修室第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 抉择余波表 / 选项 2

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、叶莲娜

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 叶莲娜把发射机的频率留给你，却没有交出自己的监听设备。你记下排污路线，庄园入口已经找到。

    English

    Yelena leaves the transmitter frequency with you but keeps her listening gear. You copy the drainage route; the manor entrance is found.

  2. 她留在秘密广播点继续监听。你独自离开，松林庄园已经解锁，但叶莲娜不会加入队伍。

    English

    She stays at the hidden broadcast room to keep listening. You leave alone: Pine Manor is unlocked, but Yelena does not join the crew.

松林庄园第三幕 / 松林庄园 · 二号恒温库 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、茹科夫、维克多

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 快门「咔嚓」一响，在轰鸣的炉口旁清脆得像一发顶上膛的子弹。

    English

    The shutter clicks. Beside the roaring furnace it sounds like a round being chambered.

  2. 焚毁台账、409 批腹透液、落满全城窗台的焦油黑雪—— 现在，它们都在你胸口的那卷胶卷里。

    English

    The burn ledger, Batch 409, the tar-black snow on every windowsill in town. All of it is now on the roll of film against your chest.

  3. 你把两袋还烫手的腹透液塞进了背包。 602 室的那盏灯，可以多亮几天了。

    English

    You stuff two bags of fluid, still hot, into your pack. The lamp in Room 602 can stay lit a few days longer.

失母后的黑市暂住第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 电台里传来一串断续的调度呼号。阿列克谢把频率抄进工作日志，随后关掉发射机——这间废信号室只供暂住一夜。

    English

    A broken dispatch call sign crackles over the radio. Aleksei copies the frequency into his logbook, then switches the set off. The abandoned signal room is only a one-night refuge.

  2. 天亮后，他会回 602，看看床边那条围巾。拉娜的事还没完。

    English

    At dawn he will go back to Room 602 to see the scarf by the bed. Lana’s case is still open.

父女对峙第三幕 / 松林庄园 · 二号恒温库 / 现场与余波 / 拍照／叶莲娜同行

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、茹科夫、叶莲娜

庄园战役选择确认后的过场 查看地图

  1. 通行证磕在杯托上，茶水漫过杯沿。「刚才那车透析液推进炉子时，你闻见了吗？外头有人为半勺麦糊告密。你把救命的药烧了，让我在这二十六度的屋子里喝茶。」

    English

    The pass hits the saucer. Tea spills over the rim. “Did you smell the dialysis fluid when they pushed it into the furnace? Outside, people inform on each other for half a spoonful of mash. You burned life-saving medicine so I could drink tea in this room at twenty-six degrees.”

  2. 茹科夫把杯子顿在桌上，杯沿裂了。「你以为这二十六度是怎么来的？门外那三万人进来，先拆暖气，再来扯你身上这件大衣。我划掉那些名字，就是为了让你还有命站在这里。」

    English

    Zhukov puts the cup down hard enough to crack the rim. “Where do you think those twenty-six degrees came from? If those thirty thousand get in, first they’ll tear out the radiators. Then they’ll tear that coat off your back. I crossed out those names to keep you standing here.”

  3. 叶莲娜把他的笔尖按向空白的一行。「门是我开的，水是我递的，4.17 MHz 的短波也是我架的。你说划掉的名字就是死人。好，写叶莲娜。盖你的红章，叫他们把我也装进第三节。」

    English

    Yelena guides his pen to an empty line. “I opened the door. I brought them water. I set up the 4.17 MHz transmitter. You said a crossed-out name was a dead person. Go on. Write Yelena. Stamp it red and have them put me in Boxcar Three.”

  4. 笔尖悬着，墨在纸上洇开。茹科夫的手往铃边挪了一寸，又停下。叶莲娜松开手：「阿列克谢，趁现在。」

    English

    The nib hangs there. Ink spreads into the paper. Zhukov’s hand moves an inch toward the bell, then stops. Yelena lets go. “Aleksei. Now.”

西站发车令第四幕 / 西站调车场 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、随行伙伴

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 23:00。鹰嘴崖死线。第三节车厢，核载 176 人。 死令：实毁数与账面数必须绝对闭合。

    English

    23:00. The Eagle's Crag line. Boxcar 3, 176 persons. Standing order: the physical count must equal the ledger.

  2. 报废守车的工具箱底，躺着一支拆开的旧莫辛纳甘。枪托裂了，枪机抹着黄油。 你把枪重新装好，枪膛里还剩三发。

    English

    At the bottom of the dead brake van's toolbox lies a disassembled Mosin–Nagant. Its stock is split, and the bolt is greased. You fit it together; three rounds remain.

  3. 所有的数字终于严丝合缝地扣上了。 一百七十六条退了烧的性命——其中一个，叫拉娜。

    English

    Every number finally locks into place. One hundred seventy-six people whose fever has broken. One of them is called Lana.

庆功标语与矿下录音第四幕 / 3 号矿坑 · 温压爆破作业面 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、矿工（录音）

首次靠近矿坑爆破断面 查看地图

  1. 红漆庆功标语刷在爆破面上。旁边的测绘工具箱翻倒了，一台摔裂的便携磁带记录器停在最后几分钟。

    English

    A red-painted victory slogan spans the blast face. A surveyor's toolbox lies overturned beside it, with a cracked portable tape recorder stopped in its final minutes.

  2. 阿列克谢按下回放键。钻机声和碎石声里，一个男人咳着喊：「瓦尼亚，别管我，带你弟跑！」

    English

    Aleksei presses play. Through the drill and falling rock, a man coughs out, "Vanya, leave me. Take your brother and run!"

  3. 录音里的另一个声音：「打穿了。山里冒灰烟了。」远处有人咳嗽，没人答话。

    English

    Another voice on the tape: "We broke through. There's gray smoke in the mountain." Someone coughs in the distance. No one answers.

  4. 「不是煤粉……吸进去，胸口就烧。」回放停在一阵电流杂音里。阿列克谢望向断面上的灰黑粉尘。

    English

    "It is not coal dust... it burns in your chest." The playback ends in static. Aleksei looks toward the gray-black powder in the exposed seam.

鹰嘴崖进场第五幕 / 鹰嘴崖 · 山谷检修线 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 阿列克谢扳开变形的门闩，侧翻的第三节车厢终于露出一道缝。能走的人一个接一个踏上检修路，崖顶的灯追在身后。

    English

    Aleksei works the buckled latch until Boxcar Three opens a gap. Everyone who can walk takes the maintenance path, with the ridge lights hunting behind them.

  2. 拉娜在人群里认出了你。最后一批人翻过护坡时，302 台的灯在远处亮了起来。

    English

    Lana recognizes you in the crowd. As the last of them clear the embankment, the lamp of Tower 302 kindles in the distance.

鹰嘴崖进场第五幕 / 鹰嘴崖 · 山谷检修线 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 2

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 阿列克谢扳开变形的门闩，侧翻的第三节车厢终于露出一道缝。能走的人一个接一个踏上检修路，崖顶的灯追在身后。

    English

    Aleksei works the buckled latch until Boxcar Three opens a gap. Everyone who can walk takes the maintenance path, with the ridge lights hunting behind them.

  2. 底单上的 0420 还在，多出来的姓名却被你留在了崖下。《工作日志》的背面又添了一道划痕。

    English

    0420 is still on the copy, but the names you left behind stay under the cliff. One more stroke marks the back of the logbook.

打开第三节车厢第五幕 / 鹰嘴崖 · 山谷检修线 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜、车厢幸存者

搜索第三节车厢目标后，撤离前必须选择 查看地图

  1. 手电扫过门框。黄铜铭牌上打着 004。清漆下那道紫黑色的笔迹，是拉娜失踪前十天，你亲手签下的。

    English

    Your flashlight crosses the frame. 004 is stamped into the brass. The violet-black signature under the varnish is the one you put there ten days before Lana disappeared.

  2. 有人抱着孩子，有人用铁饭盒接门缝飘进的雪。拉娜从车厢里扣住你的手腕，朝门槛里面拉了一把。手背上的灰鳞掉光了，新皮冻得通红。

    English

    Someone holds a child. Someone catches the drifting snow in a mess tin. From inside the car, Lana grips your wrist and pulls you across the threshold. The scales are gone; the new skin is red with cold.

  3. 拉娜先看见你大衣上的铁路工牌，挂绳是她缝过的。「哥，我听见你在门外喊我，还以为是梦。门边的几个能走。里头……还有人在喘气。」

    English

    Lana sees the railway badge first. She mended the cord herself. “Alyosha. I heard you calling from outside and thought it was a dream. The ones by the door can walk. Inside… I can still hear people breathing.”

  4. 莫罗佐夫，认得 004 上的签名吗？放下枪，把底单交出来。我可以把你那笔调拨改成戴罪立功，给你和拉娜两张过卡证，让你们活着离开这条线。你不肯？那就和车里的人一起算：你会被登记成盗枪、劫车的叛乱者；这 176 个已经销账的人，天亮前一个也不会剩下。各肃清小队就位！核对第三节车厢档案号 0401 至 0576！准备投掷铝热剂燃烧弹，就地清除！

    English

    Morozov, do you recognize the signature on 004? Lower your rifle and hand over the dispatch copy. I can recast your transfer order as proof of cooperation and issue passes for you and Lana, letting you both leave this line alive. Refuse, and you will be counted with the people in that car: a gun thief, a train hijacker, a rebel. Those 176 people have already been struck from the books; not one will be left by dawn. OSK squads, take your positions. Verify files 0401 through 0576 in the third car. Prepare thermite charges. Eliminate them here.

    必选 · 播完后继续夜潜，撤离成功才结算

    选项与回执
    1. 带能走的人一起沿检修线撤离
      English

      Take everyone who can walk along the service line

    2. 先带拉娜和底单撤离
      English

      Leave first with Lana and the dispatch copy

交接底单第五幕 / 布拉风货场 · 南侧编组线 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

带回交接底单后标记检修线 查看地图

  1. 检修柜里的底单没有销账章。车里的人还不能被账本判死。

    English

    The copy in the maintenance locker carries no closure stamp. The ledger cannot declare the people aboard dead.

  2. 底单与西站发车令对上了。你把通往鹰嘴崖的检修线记在地图上。

    English

    The copy matches the West Yard dispatch order. You mark the maintenance route to Eagle's Crag on the map.

焊死的单元门第五幕 / 第 3 居住区楼梯间 / 现场与余波 / 居民破门

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、柳德米拉、佳娜、居民

开启鹰嘴崖行动前，按同行者选页

  1. 楼梯间灌满黑烟。柳德米拉被挤倒，仍把佳娜护在怀里，手里攥着一张熏黑的领粮券。墙上的喇叭还在叫人留在屋里。

    English

    Black smoke fills the stairwell. Lyudmila is knocked down but still shields Jana, clutching a soot-blackened ration ticket. The wall speaker keeps telling residents to stay inside.

  2. 柳德米拉把佳娜推到你怀里，把那张熏黑的领粮券也塞过来：「我吸进太多烟，腿使不上劲，走不出去了。带佳娜走。」佳娜抓住你的袖口：「姥姥说把门焊上就不漏风了……可楼道里都是烟。叔叔，门还能打开吗？」

    English

    Lyudmila pushes Jana into your arms and presses the soot-blackened ration ticket into your hand. “I've breathed in too much smoke. My legs won't carry me out. Take Jana.” Jana clutches your sleeve: “Grandma said welding the door shut would keep the wind out… but the stairwell is full of smoke. Uncle, can we still open the door?”

  3. 几个住户把撬棍和铁管顶进门缝，一起压下去。门轴松开时，后面的人扶住了佳娜。

    English

    Residents wedge crowbars and iron pipe into the door seam and lean together. As the hinge gives, someone behind them catches Jana.

  4. 没人欢呼。住户先把伤者扶到背风处，挨个叫邻居的名字。火沿楼道往上爬，熟悉的窗子一扇扇亮起，又被黑烟吞掉。有人认出自家的窗，往前走了半步，身边的人拉住了她。阿列克谢攥着那张熏黑的领粮券，孩子还在咳，玻璃在火里炸裂。

    English

    No one cheers. Residents first move the injured out of the wind and call their neighbors by name. Fire climbs the stairwell; familiar windows flare one by one, then vanish in smoke. A woman recognizes her own window and takes half a step forward before someone holds her back. Aleksei grips the soot-blackened ration ticket. A child keeps coughing; glass bursts in the fire.

  5. 门房的旧电台突然压过人声：「布拉风号在鹰嘴崖脱轨。押运目标未清点。附近肃清小队立即集合，封锁检修线；发现幸存者，就地清除。」

    English

    A battered radio in the lobby cuts through the voices: “The Buran has derailed at Eagle's Crag. The transport has not been accounted for. OSK squads, assemble. Seal the service line. Eliminate any survivors on sight.”

  6. 最后一个孩子交到邻居怀里后，阿列克谢把莫辛纳甘背带收紧，检查枪机。伤员留在门房，住户拿起手电和手边的工具，跟着他沿铁路赶往鹰嘴崖。

    English

    After the last child is placed in a neighbor's arms, Aleksei tightens the Mosin's sling and checks the bolt. The injured stay in the lobby; residents take flashlights and whatever tools they can carry, then follow him along the railway toward Eagle's Crag.

  3. 帕维尔顶着浓烟割开焊死的横梁。火星落在他的袖口，他把最后一处焊点烧断。

    English

  4. 没人欢呼。住户先把伤者扶到背风处，挨个叫邻居的名字。火沿楼道往上爬，熟悉的窗子一扇扇亮起，又被黑烟吞掉。有人认出自家的窗，往前走了半步，身边的人拉住了她。阿列克谢攥着那张熏黑的领粮券，孩子还在咳，玻璃在火里炸裂。

  5. 门房的旧电台突然压过人声：「布拉风号在鹰嘴崖脱轨。押运目标未清点。附近肃清小队立即集合，封锁检修线；发现幸存者，就地清除。」

    English

    After the last child is placed in a neighbor's arms, Aleksei tightens the Mosin's sling and checks the bolt. Pavel shoulders his pipe wrench. The injured stay in the lobby, while residents follow them along the railway toward Eagle's Crag.

302 广播塔第六幕 / 302 高地 · 广播塔 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 1

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜

静态余波表。部分旧选项已由现场过场替代，触发以游戏入口为准。 查看地图

  1. 名单一个名字一个名字地念下去。全城的收音机里，第一次有了他们的姓名，不再只是编号。

    English

    The list is read out name by name. For the first time the city's radios carry their names, not just file numbers.

  2. 南门守住了。天亮时 302 台的发射机还在响；有人在信里写下了听到的第一个名字。

    English

    The south gate holds. At dawn the transmitter is still alive, and someone writes down the first name they heard.

302 广播塔第六幕 / 302 高地 · 广播塔 / 抉择余波表 / 选项 2

  1. 照片和编号上了频率。发射机继续响着，雪从敞开的塔门落进来。

    English

    Photographs and file numbers go out. The transmitter keeps humming as snow drifts through the open tower door.

  2. 城市听见了罪证，却不知道该为谁记下名字。名单留在你口袋里，仍旧只是一张纸。

    English

    The city hears the evidence but not whom to mourn by name. The list stays in your pocket, still only paper.

602 室证据交接第六幕 / 602 室 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

  1. 你把发车令、未销账的交接底单和焚化台账照片摊在 602 室的桌上。三份证据终于指向同一条路：让全城听见。

    English

    You lay the dispatch order, unclosed handover copy, and furnace-ledger photograph on the Room 602 table. The three pieces of evidence point toward one next step: let the whole city hear.

广播完成第六幕 / 302 高地 · 广播塔 / 现场与余波 / 阿列克谢／只播档案号

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、听众

完成播报后，按操作者及公开范围分流 查看地图

  1. 阿列克谢合上总闸。噪声一起，拉娜用双手扶住话筒。 拉娜的手按在名单上。阿列克谢读出「零四二零」，翻过了这一页。

    English

    Aleksei closes the main switch. When the static comes up, Lana steadies the microphone with both hands. Lana keeps her hand on the list. Aleksei reads “zero four two zero” and turns the page.

  2. 听众知道罪证已经播出，却没有听到可以写下的姓名。铅笔停在空白纸上。

    English

    Listeners hear that the evidence is out, but no names to write down. The pencil pauses over the blank page.

广播完成第六幕 / 302 高地 · 广播塔 / 现场与余波 / 阿列克谢／播姓名

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、听众

完成播报后，按操作者及公开范围分流 查看地图

  1. 阿列克谢合上总闸。噪声一起，拉娜用双手扶住话筒。 电流声吞了第一个音。拉娜等它过去，重新念了一遍：「拉娜·莫罗佐娃。档案号 0420。」

    English

    Aleksei closes the main switch. When the static comes up, Lana steadies the microphone with both hands. Static swallows the first sound. Lana waits, then starts again. “Lana Morozova. File 0420.”

  2. 街对面有人把收音机搬到窗边，在旧配给单背面写下「拉娜」。铅笔停了一下，又添上她的姓。

    English

    Across the street, someone brings a radio to the window and writes “Lana” on the back of a ration slip. The pencil pauses, then adds her surname.

广播完成第六幕 / 302 高地 · 广播塔 / 现场与余波 / 帕维尔／只播档案号

  1. 帕维尔撑住开关，阿列克谢对着话筒把证词送上频率。 拉娜的手按在名单上。阿列克谢读出「零四二零」，翻过了这一页。

    English

    Pavel holds the switch while Aleksei sends the testimony over the air. Lana keeps her hand on the list. Aleksei reads “zero four two zero” and turns the page.

  2. 听众知道罪证已经播出，却没有听到可以写下的姓名。铅笔停在空白纸上。

    English

    Listeners hear that the evidence is out, but no names to write down. The pencil pauses over the blank page.

扎哈尔离队条件余波 / 清杀队岗亭 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、扎哈尔

  1. 扎哈尔不再跟在你身后。岗亭的检查灯还亮着，日志没有记下他后来去了哪边。

    English

    Zakhar no longer walks behind you. The checkpoint lamp is still on, and the log does not say which way he went.

602 室的空床条件余波 / 602 室 / 现场与余波

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

  1. 你回到 602 室时，床已经空了。围巾叠在床沿，停下来的透析机没有留下回声。

    English

    When you return to Room 602, the bed is empty. The scarf rests at its edge; the stopped dialysis machine leaves no echo.

夜潜地图

第3居住区 · 锅炉房后巷地图 / 夜景与日景

楼下就能摸到，煤堆旁常有人堆杂物。 两只旧木箱、一台报废蒸汽泵。清杀队偶尔从东侧栅栏路过。

查看日景 · 场景文件

城北四号铁路冷库地图 / 夜景与日景

据说封着一批医疗耗材，也冻死过人。 南库 3 号柱后有人求救。冷库铁门可以撬棍卡死。

查看日景 · 场景文件

夜间广轨货场地图 / 夜景与日景

编组表和废件堆都在那，灯也最亮。 两座煤水塔可躲探照灯；清杀队 2 人一组巡逻。

查看日景 · 场景文件

铁道下穿积冰道地图 / 夜景与日景

黑冰很滑，声音会传得很远。有人在这儿烧火。 涉雪泥移动 ×0.65，噪声 ×2.0；火堆可回温。

查看日景 · 场景文件

东街工人食堂 · 后厨地图 / 夜景与日景

账面已清空的老楼，可里面还有十九只碗。 备餐台砧板上插着砍骨刀；窄门会被冲开。

查看日景 · 场景文件

旧传染病疗养院 · 地下室地图 / 夜景与日景

有人被皮带捆在铁架床上熬过了第四天。 地底铁笼区。需要至少 1 份医疗物资与 2 份情报线索。

查看日景 · 场景文件

铁路黑市（废弃工人镇）地图 / 夜景与日景

拿东西换东西。别相信任何人递过来的手。 人会开枪，但比清杀队讲道理。可交易情报。

查看日景 · 场景文件

松林庄园 · 二号恒温库地图 / 夜景与日景

墙外 −35℃，墙里 26℃。409 批腹透液就在那儿。 电网高墙 + 特勤值守。被发现基本等于死。

查看日景 · 场景文件

西站调车场地图 / 夜景与日景

报废守车的工具箱里有支旧步枪，调度室外贴着今晚的发车令。 机车水箱里可能塞着手稿复写件。

查看日景 · 场景文件

3 号矿坑 · 温压爆破作业面地图 / 夜景与日景

灰鳞病的源头就埋在断面里。矿上还码着铝热剂。 留守技术员博伊科守着起爆器；他手上的鳞屑正在掉。

查看日景 · 场景文件

白桦林废弃板房地图 / 夜景与日景

风小，能捡到柴。偶尔有逃出来的人躲在这儿。 低风险补给点，适合缺体温的夜里。

查看日景 · 场景文件

机修车间地图 / 夜景与日景

车床边散落着零件，焊机还能打着火。 车间里工具多，但巡逻队常来查。

查看日景 · 场景文件

温室大棚地图 / 夜景与日景

塑料布还算严实，冻土里埋着去年的土豆。 低风险食物点，适合缺粮的夜里。

查看日景 · 场景文件

档案馆电报局地图 / 夜景与日景

旧电报和花名册都在，值钱的情报也在。 高风险：清杀队重点盯防的情报点。

查看日景 · 场景文件

布拉风货场 · 南侧编组线地图 / 夜景与日景

货场里还有一份没盖注销章的交接底单。 站房探照灯沿月台扫动；布拉风号尾部的检修柜可能留有底单。

查看日景 · 场景文件

鹰嘴崖 · 山谷检修线地图 / 夜景与日景

盘山线在悬崖边急转，谷底有一条旧检修道。 沿检修路靠近车厢，崖顶探照灯和巡逻兵都在找幸存者。

查看日景 · 场景文件

302 高地 · 广播塔地图 / 夜景与日景

拉娜和幸存者需要一个能让全城听见的频率。 信号机组在塔顶；南门是唯一可撤离的出口。需要鹰嘴崖路线和庄园罪证。

结局与余波

A 留在 602结局 / 602 室 / 结局

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、卡佳

无真相证据，母亲在世

  1. 你把工作日志合上，坐回母亲床边。她伸手来摸你的手套，问拉娜有没有来信。

    English

    You close the logbook and sit beside Mother. She reaches for your glove and asks whether Lana has written.

  2. 窗台又积了一层雪。你把收音机拧小，等昨晚那段公报再念一遍。

    English

    Another layer of snow settles on the sill. You turn down the radio and wait for last night's bulletin to repeat.

B 荒诞解放结局 / 302 高地 · 广播塔 / 结局 / 旧档未记录撤离范围

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜

真相在手，划痕不少于 5 查看地图

  1. 发射机还在响。拉娜从门口回头看你，手搭在门框上。

    English

    The transmitter is still running. Lana looks back at you from the doorway, her hand on the frame.

  2. 你关上发射机，拿起大衣。拉娜在门外等你，你们一前一后下了楼。

    English

    You switch off the transmitter and pick up your coat. Lana waits outside, and you follow her down the stairs.

B 荒诞解放结局 / 302 高地 · 广播塔 / 结局 / 带能走的人

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜

真相在手，划痕不少于 5 查看地图

  1. 广播停了。拉娜把话筒放回桌上，听完最后一阵电流声才走出门。

    English

    The broadcast ends. Lana sets the microphone down and waits for the last crackle of static before leaving.

  2. 拉娜沿着山路追上一个拎着饭盒的人。她在车厢里替他接过雪水，这回叫出了他的名字。你赶上来时，她给你让了半步。

    English

    Lana catches up to a man carrying a mess tin. She caught snow for him in the car. This time she calls him by name. When you reach her, she moves half a step aside.

B 荒诞解放结局 / 302 高地 · 广播塔 / 结局 / 带拉娜和底单

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜

真相在手，划痕不少于 5 查看地图

  1. 广播塔的红灯熄了。拉娜等到走廊上只剩下你们两个人，才停下脚步。

    English

    The tower's red light goes out. Lana waits until the two of you are alone in the corridor, then stops.

  2. 拉娜望着窗外的雪。 「哥，咱们走的时候……第三节车门还开着吗？」 阿列克谢：「开着。」

    English

    Lana: “When we left, was the door to Boxcar Three still open?” Aleksei: “It was.”

C 麻木结局 / 602 室与地窖 / 结局

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

无真相证据，母亲离世

  1. 你把没查完的《工作日志》合上。床边的围巾没有动，透析机也不再发出声音。

    English

    You close the unfinished logbook. The scarf by the empty bed has not moved, and the dialysis machine is silent.

  2. 他独自从排污涵洞爬回空地窖。雪盖住名字，也盖住了最后一点反抗。

    English

    He crawls alone from the drain into an empty cellar. Snow covers the names and the last trace of resistance.

  3. 地窖里有根水管在滴。你数到第四声，放下水碗，把那本没写完的日志推进床底。

    English

    A pipe drips in the cellar. On the fourth drop you set down your bowl and push the unfinished logbook under the bed.

α 你好，拉娜结局 / 602 室 / 结局

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜、卡佳

真相在手，母亲在世，划痕少于 5，未被出卖；零划痕追加奖励对白与分镜

  1. 拉娜回到了 602。母亲还在床边等他们，三个人终于重新站在同一盏灯下。

    English

    Lana returns to Room 602. Mother is waiting beside the bed, and all three are together beneath the same lamp again.

  2. 拉娜把热水递给母亲。你把钢笔放在交接底单旁。母亲摸到女儿手背上的新皮，拇指停在那里：「拉娜，杯子烫，慢点。」

    English

    Lana hands Mother a cup of warm water. You lay the pen beside the handover copy. Mother feels the new skin on the back of Lana’s hand and leaves her thumb there. “Lana. The cup is hot. Slowly.”

β 荒原之火结局 / 城外雪原 / 结局

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜

真相在手，划痕少于 5 且不满足 α；零划痕追加奖励对白与分镜

  1. 真相和拉娜都带出了城，母亲却没能等到他们。荒原的火烧过漫长一夜。

    English

    The truth and Lana make it out of the city, but Mother does not live to see them. The outland fire burns through the long night.

  2. 天快亮了。拉娜把档案塞进大衣里，接过母亲的围巾。她结了两次才系紧，第二次把那截磨薄的线头塞进了领口。

    English

    Dawn is close. Lana tucks the records into her coat and takes Mother’s scarf. It takes her two tries to tie it. The second time she slips the worn end inside her collar.

β 荒原之火结局 / 城外雪原 / 结局 / 母亲存活

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜

真相在手，划痕少于 5 且不满足 α；零划痕追加奖励对白与分镜

  1. 母亲还活着。真相已经传开，出卖他们的人也把消息送到了清杀队。

    English

    Mother is alive. The truth is out, and someone has passed word to the OSK.

  2. 城外火光下，拉娜和阿列克谢分着守夜。他们等天亮，再找路回去。

    English

    Outside the city, Lana and Aleksei take turns watching the fire. At dawn they will look for a way back.

α 你好，拉娜结局 / 602 室 / 结局 / 零划痕奖励对白（划痕 0）

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜、卡佳

真相在手，母亲在世，划痕少于 5，未被出卖；零划痕追加奖励对白与分镜

  1. 拉娜回到了 602。母亲还在床边等他们，三个人终于重新站在同一盏灯下。

    English

    Lana returns to Room 602. Mother is waiting beside the bed, and all three are together beneath the same lamp again.

  2. 拉娜把热水递给母亲。你把钢笔放在交接底单旁。母亲摸到女儿手背上的新皮，拇指停在那里：「拉娜，杯子烫，慢点。」

    English

    Lana hands Mother a cup of warm water. You lay the pen beside the handover copy. Mother feels the new skin on the back of Lana’s hand and leaves her thumb there. “Lana. The cup is hot. Slowly.”

  3. 母亲睡下后，拉娜翻到工作日志背面。纸页空白，台灯把纸边照得发黄。 拉娜：「你没往这儿写。」 阿列克谢：「有些事，我还记得。」 她把日志合上，放回床头灯下：「那就记着。明天还要把名字补完。」

    English

    After Mother falls asleep, Lana turns to the back of the logbook. The page is blank, its edges yellow beneath the lamp. Lana: “You left this page empty.” Aleksei: “Some things I still remember.” She closes the book and sets it by the lamp. “Then remember. We still have names to write tomorrow.”

β 荒原之火结局 / 城外雪原 / 结局 / 零划痕奖励对白（划痕 0）

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、拉娜

真相在手，划痕少于 5 且不满足 α；零划痕追加奖励对白与分镜

  1. 真相和拉娜都带出了城，母亲却没能等到他们。荒原的火烧过漫长一夜。

    English

    The truth and Lana make it out of the city, but Mother does not live to see them. The outland fire burns through the long night.

  2. 天快亮了。拉娜把档案塞进大衣里，接过母亲的围巾。她结了两次才系紧，第二次把那截磨薄的线头塞进了领口。

    English

    Dawn is close. Lana tucks the records into her coat and takes Mother’s scarf. It takes her two tries to tie it. The second time she slips the worn end inside her collar.

  3. 火堆只剩一圈暗红。拉娜翻到工作日志背面，空白纸页映着火光。 拉娜：「一笔也没留下。」 阿列克谢：「空白不是清白。」 拉娜把本子递回来：「我知道。可你把它带出了城。明天，我们把那些名字送到北边。」

    English

    Only a dark red ring remains in the fire. Lana opens the back of the logbook; the blank page catches its light. Lana: “Not a single stroke.” Aleksei: “A blank page is not absolution.” Lana hands it back. “I know. But you brought it out of the city. Tomorrow, we take those names north.”

帕沙的防化服结局余波 / 鹰嘴崖 · 山谷检修线 / 结局 / 满足帕沙余波条件时附加

出场或被记录人物　帕沙、无名女子、阿列克谢

帕沙目击与鹰嘴崖存活旗标同时成立 查看地图

  1. 帕沙把防化服拉到那个女人肩上。扣子试了两次都没对上。 帕沙：「大哥……帮我系上。系严实点，外头雪大。」

    English

    Pasha pulls his protective coat over the woman's shoulders. Twice he misses the buttonhole. Pasha: “Brother… help me button it. All the way. It's snowing hard.”

对白与底稿

账面死人第一幕 / 城北四号铁路冷库 / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、切尔诺夫、谢尔盖、玛丽亚（遗体）、灰烬

查看地图

  1. 旁白 / 阿列克谢 白昼。城北四号铁路冷库。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 挂满白霜的氨气管道在头顶纵横交错，地面结了一层油污与冰渣混合的黑硬壳。阿列克谢作为征召搬运工，正将一箱箱贴着内务部封条的黄油和咸鱼搬上平板手推车。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 在南库 3 号水泥柱后面，一个穿着破烂黑棉袄的男人正跪在冰水沟旁，怀里紧紧裹着旧军毯里的小孩。孩子一只僵白的小手露在毯子外，嘴唇冻得发紫，胸口没有起伏。男人把那只手按在自己胸口，想把身上剩下的热气分给她。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 内务部巡逻队的皮靴声在铁格栅通道上停下。切尔诺夫中士停在柱子前，手里拿着便携手持打卡机，金属探头对准了男人的手背。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 终端滴地响了一声，红灯闪烁。

    切尔诺夫中士 （语气平直，不带情绪） 档案 0892，谢尔盖。昨日 22:00 移交单乌拉尔山线。主脑记录：已出清。当前坐标在册人数：0。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 男人猛地抬起头，眼睛里全是红血丝，干裂的嘴唇裂开血口子。

    谢尔盖 长官，机器搞错了。我昨天还在锅炉房换班……玛丽亚昨夜就没了。给我块布，让我把她裹好。我不能把她一个人留在这儿。

    切尔诺夫中士 （把终端翻转过来，屏幕对着男人的脸） 上面写着零。机器只认打孔卡。在册是零，这根柱子底下就没有人。没有人，就没有今天的配给。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 切尔诺夫伸手推开配给箱的铁盖，锁栓卡嗒一声落进锁槽。男人扑上去抓住中士的裤脚，指甲在硬皮靴上划出刺耳的声响。

    谢尔盖 长官，求你……至少给我块布。我得把她带走。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 切尔诺夫中士往后撤了半步。他没有拔枪，也没有踹人，只是机械地把男人的手指一根根掰开。在转身离开的瞬间，切尔诺夫的脚尖在雪地里微微一滞，踢开了一个碍事的空铁皮盒，低声自言自语般吐出半句话：

    切尔诺夫中士 ……别在这儿耗着。去西站水塔后头找捡煤渣的，兴许有烂土豆。死在冷库里，连拉尸车都不会来。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 中士带着两名队员快步走远。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢站在三米外，手里的板车把手被他捏得咯咯作响。在水泥柱后的阴影里，一只缺了半只耳朵的灰狼犬正趴在废弃收发亭的台阶下。它脖颈上缠着被剪断的锈铁丝；铁丝曾经勒进皮肉，留下了一圈褪不掉的伤痕，周围的毛也长得稀疏。它没有靠近谢尔盖，只安静地看着阿列克谢。

    English

    Daylight. Rail Cold Storage No.4, north of the city.

    Ammonia pipes crisscrossed overhead, thick with white frost; underfoot the floor was crusted with a black hard shell of oil and ice. Aleksei, a conscripted porter, was loading crates of butter and salt fish, sealed with Ministry of Internal Affairs strips, onto a flat handcart.

    Behind concrete column 3 in the south hall, a man in a ragged black padded coat knelt beside a gutter of ice water, holding a child tightly wrapped in an old army blanket. The child's face was grey, its lips blue-purple with cold.

    The boots of a Ministry patrol stopped on the iron-grate walkway. Sergeant Chernov halted before the column, a portable punch-clock in his hand, its metal probe aimed at the man's hand.

    The terminal gave a single beep; a red light blinked.

    File 0892, Sergey. Transferred to the Ural Line Station yesterday at 22:00. Central record: cleared. Persons registered at this coordinate: zero.

    The man's head snapped up, his eyes threaded with red, his cracked lips split and bleeding.

    Officer... the machine's wrong! I never boarded — I was covering a shift at the boiler house yesterday, the child had a fever... I'm not dead, look, Maria's not dead either, she's still breathing!

    It says zero up there. The machine reads punch cards. Zero on the register means nobody under this column. Nobody means no ration today.

    Chernov reached over and pushed the iron lid of the ration crate shut; the bolt clicked into its slot. The man lunged and clutched the sergeant's trouser leg, his nails scraping a shrill sound across the stiff leather boot.

    Officer! One mouthful — just one spoon of hot soup for Maria...

    Sergeant Chernov stepped back half a pace. He did not draw his pistol or kick; he only pried the man's fingers loose, one by one, mechanically. As he turned to go, his toe faltered a moment in the snow, kicked aside an empty tin box in the way, and he let fall half a sentence, almost to himself:

    ...Don't waste time here. Go behind the water tower at West Station, find the cinder-pickers; there might be rotten potatoes. Die in the cold store and not even the corpse cart will come.

    The sergeant strode off with his two men.

    Aleksei stood three metres away, the handcart's grip creaking under his fingers. In the shadow behind the concrete column, a grey wolfdog with half an ear missing lay on the steps of the abandoned dispatch hut. A length of cut, rusted wire was twisted round its neck; it had once bitten into the flesh and left a ring of scar that would not fade, the fur around it grown thin. It did not go to Sergey, only watched Aleksei quietly.

    选项与回执
    1. 帮谢尔盖带走玛丽亚，喂灰犬并记下两人的名字

      你把黑麦饼塞进谢尔盖手里。他没吃，只把玛丽亚往怀里搂紧。你在日志上记下：谢尔盖还活着，玛丽亚昨夜冻死了。你扶谢尔盖站起，替他托住女儿的腿。剩下的碎屑喂给灰犬；剪断锈铁丝后，它自己跟上了你们。

      English

      Help Sergey carry Maria out, feed the wolfdog, and record their names You put the rye bread in Sergey's hand. He does not eat; he only holds Maria closer. You record that Sergey is alive and Maria froze to death last night. You help him stand and support his daughter's legs. The wolfdog eats the crumbs; once freed from the wire, it chooses to follow.

    2. 不帮谢尔盖，低头搬你的箱子

      你低头推车，没有帮谢尔盖，也没有喂那只灰犬。谢尔盖还抱着玛丽亚跪在三号柱后。灰犬留在收发亭旁，颈上的旧伤还在；它没有跟上。

      English

      Leave Sergey behind and keep hauling crates. You keep hauling without helping Sergey or feeding the wolfdog. Sergey remains on his knees behind column three, holding Maria. The dog stays by the dispatch hut, its old neck wound still visible. It does not follow.

铁壳号编组单第一幕 / 夜间广轨货场 / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、扎哈尔

查看地图

  1. 旁白 / 阿列克谢 货场换岗时，阿列克谢从煤水塔后绕过一排待报废的配给箱。调度箱背面贴着一张受潮的编组单：「铁壳号 · 第 7 次特别运输 · 第三节闷罐 · 档案 0401—0576」。0420 在这个区间里。终到栏只印了「乌拉尔山线 · 销账点」。一个戴蓝袖章的核算员正把没收来的手套、滤芯和烟草重新分堆，手里那本账簿没有一页写着失主的名字。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢踩响一块冻裂的冰壳。扎哈尔猛地抬头，正往棉袄里塞的滤芯掉在雪上。远处巡逻灯扫过来；他看了阿列克谢一眼，脸上的血色退了个干净。

    核算员扎哈尔 （把账本紧紧按在身前，目光不时扫向巡逻灯） 别喊巡逻队。我叫扎哈尔。以前我替配给站登记每一袋粮，后来那本账上只剩空格。你不把这只滤芯的事报上去，我就跟你走，替你认废料、找补给。别把这当忠诚——我只是不想再挨饿。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢问他，如果清杀队愿意拿配额换一份举报，他会怎么做。扎哈尔没有否认，只用拇指擦掉账本封面上的冻泥。

    核算员扎哈尔 要是他们给的配额比你给的多，我会把知道的都报上去。账不会替谁讲情。

    English

    In the water tower's shadow, a consist sheet rots against the back of a switch box:

    [BURAN · SPECIAL RUN 7 · BOXCAR 03 · FILES 0401–0576 · URALS LINE · CLOSURE POINT]

    You fold it into your inner pocket. Lana is 0420. She is inside that range.

    Your heel crunches the frozen snow. A filter slips from the coat Zakhar was stuffing it into. He sees you and goes pale as a patrol light swings this way.

    Don't call the patrol. My name is Zakhar. I used to record every sack at the ration office; in the end, my ledger had only blanks. If you don't report this filter, I'll come with you and help identify salvage and find supplies. Don't call it loyalty. I just don't want to go hungry again.

    选项与回执
    1. 不举报扎哈尔，收好编组单（+1 情报）

      你没有把那只滤芯交给巡逻队。扎哈尔扯下蓝臂章，把扩音器塞进棉袄，跟上了你。那张编组单隔着衬衣贴在肋骨上：0420 在第三节里等着你。

      English

      Do not report Zakhar; pocket the consist (+1 Intel) You do not turn Zakhar in over the filter. He tears off his blue armband, hides the loudhailer under his coat and follows. The consist presses against your ribs: 0420 is waiting in the third car.

假人哨塔第一幕 / 西站调车场 / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、帕沙

查看地图

  1. 阿列克谢 西站调车场的假人哨塔下，一个年轻新兵烧得发抖。探照灯还在转，他手里的体温计停在三十九度二。

    帕沙 大哥……你有退烧药吗？明早督察队要量体温。过了三十八度五，挂上哨塔的就会是我。

    阿列克谢 你是清杀队的人。可你现在只是个发着烧、想回家的孩子。

    English

    Beneath the dummy watchtower at the West Yard, a young conscript shakes with fever. The searchlight still turns; his thermometer reads 39.2.

    Brother... do you have anything for a fever? The inspectors take temperatures at dawn. Above 38.5, they will hang me from the tower.

    You wear an OSK coat. But right now you are only a feverish boy who wants to go home.

    选项与回执
    1. 分给帕沙一片阿司匹林

      帕沙吞下药片，体温开始往下走。他答应明早把探照灯抬向天顶。

      English

      Give Pasha one aspirin Pasha swallows the tablet and his fever begins to break. He promises to aim the searchlight skyward at dawn.

    2. 贴墙绕行，把他的哭声留给黑暗

      你退回阴影里。换岗的脚步声走近，随后响起一声短促的枪声。

      English

      Slip past and leave his crying to the dark You retreat into the shadows. The changing patrol approaches, followed by one short gunshot.

    3. 夺走他的补给

      你把帕沙按进雪里：“内务部的狗不值得救。你替他们守塔，也算帮凶。”你搜走他仅有的口粮，工作日志背面多了一道划痕。

      English

      Take his supplies You force Pasha into the snow. “OSK dogs aren't worth saving. You guard their tower; you're complicit too.” You take his last ration. Another stroke marks the back of the logbook.

东街食堂第二幕 / 东街工人食堂 · 后厨 / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、瓦西里、费奥多尔

查看地图

  1. 旁白 / 阿列克谢 东街工人食堂。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 这里早在一个月前就停止了集中供应，整栋建筑被列入“账面清空区”。但阿列克谢知道，老炊事员费奥多尔依然躲在后厨，守着地窖里仅剩的三袋陈化黑麦粒，偷偷给附近滞留的十九个老铁路工熬糊糊。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 后厨弥漫着酸败的油脂味和发馊的面粉气味。地面积着寸许厚的黑水，水面上浮着薄冰。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢挑开破烂的布帘跨进后厨。灶台边，费奥多尔老爹正蹲在地上用小煤炉煮雪水，磨旧的袖口沾着炉灰。

    费奥多尔 （抬头看了看阿列克谢） 阿廖沙……你来得不巧。锅里只有雪水。地窖快见底了。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 话音未落，储藏室那扇用粗铁丝捆着的木门突然发出了一声沉重的闷响。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 嘭！

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 整扇木门被一股巨大的力量撞得向外凸起，木屑崩落。费奥多尔手里的铁勺当啷一声掉进水盆里，脸色刷地惨白。

    费奥多尔 （声音剧烈发抖） 瓦西里……是我儿子！他前天夜里冒着大雪去给四楼瘫痪的老钳工送糊糊，半路上冻透了，回来就发起高烧……我把他锁在里头，给他灌了姜水，可他……他手上的皮全硬了……

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 木门在第二下撞击中彻底炸裂开来！

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 一个二十出头的小伙子从黑暗中跌撞着冲了出来。他浑身滚烫，呼出的白气带着刺鼻的酸臭味，右手背到小臂覆盖着一层厚厚的灰白角质硬鳞。他的双眼布满紫黑色的血翳，喉咙里发出类似风箱漏气般的急促喘息，毫无目标地挥舞着双手，一头撞翻了备餐的铁案台！

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 案台上的大铁盆砸在地上，发出震耳欲聋的巨响。瓦西里被巨响刺激，猛地扭过头，浑浊的眼睛锁定了站在门口的阿列克谢，嘶吼着扑了过来！

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢被撞得倒退数步，后背狠狠砸在冷藏柜锋利的铁角上，剧痛让他眼前一黑。瓦西里的力气大得惊人，滚烫的双手死死掐住了阿列克谢的脖子，口水和带血的泡沫滴在阿列克谢的镜片上。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 空气被一点点抽离。阿列克谢的右手在油腻的砧板上胡乱摸索，手指触到了一个冰冷沉重的木柄——那是费奥多尔砍冻猪肉用的宽背砍骨刀。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 生死只在一瞬。恐惧彻底压垮了理智。

    English

    East Street Workers' Canteen.

    Centralized food distribution here stopped a full month ago; the whole building was marked a “ledger-cleared zone.” But Aleksei knows the one-eyed old cook Fyodor is still hiding in the kitchen, guarding the last three sacks of aged rye in the cellar and quietly boiling gruel for the nineteen old railway workers stranded nearby.

    The kitchen reeks of rancid fat and sour flour. An inch of black water lies on the floor, with a skin of thin ice floating on it.

    Aleksei pushes aside the ragged cloth curtain and steps into the kitchen. By the stove, old Fyodor crouches on the floor, boiling snow water over a small coal brazier, the black leather patch over his one eye worn shiny.

    Alyosha... you've come at a bad time. There's only snow water in the pot. The cellar's nearly empty.

    Before the words are out, the storeroom door — a plank bound with coarse iron wire — gives a heavy, muffled thud.

    Bang!

    The whole door bows outward under some tremendous force, splinters flying. The iron ladle clatters out of Fyodor's hand into the washbasin, and his face goes white in an instant.

    Vasily... he's my son! The night before last he went out in the blizzard to take gruel to the paralysed old fitter on the fourth floor, and was frozen through before he got back — then the fever took him... I locked him in and gave him ginger water, but his... the skin on his hands has all gone hard...

    The door bursts completely apart on the second impact!

    A young man, barely twenty, staggers out of the darkness. He is burning hot; the white breath from his mouth carries a sharp, sour stench. His right hand and forearm are covered in a thick layer of grey-white keratinous scale. Purple-black clouds fill his eyes, and a rapid, bellows-leaking rasp works in his throat. He flails blindly and crashes headlong into the steel prep table, upsetting it.

    The great iron basin on the table hits the floor with a deafening clang. The noise jolts Vasily: his head snaps round, his clouded eyes fix on Aleksei in the doorway, and he lunges with a shriek.

    The blow drives Aleksei back several steps; his spine strikes the sharp iron corner of the cold-storage cabinet, and the pain blacks out his sight. Vasily's strength is terrifying. His scalding hands lock around Aleksei's throat, and spittle and bloody foam drip onto the lenses of his glasses.

    The air is drawn out of him bit by bit. Aleksei's right hand gropes blindly over the greasy chopping block, and his fingers close on a cold, heavy wooden handle — Fyodor's broad-backed cleaver for frozen pork.

    Life and death turn on a single instant. Fear breaks reason utterly.

    选项与回执
    1. 进入后厨，保护自己并制住瓦西里

      你推开后厨的门。费奥多尔守在瓦西里身边；刀架上的宽背砍骨刀已被你取下。

      English

      Enter the kitchen, defend yourself and stop Vasily You enter the kitchen. Fyodor stays beside Vasily; the heavy cleaver is no longer in its block.

回退手稿第二幕 / 旧传染病疗养院 · 地下室 / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、梁诺夫、古丽娅、安娜

查看地图

  1. 旁白 / 阿列克谢 旧传染病疗养院地下二层。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 这里曾经是结核病隔离病区，阴暗潮湿的水泥墙上结满了白色的硝霜。空气中弥漫着高浓度石炭酸与腐烂组织的怪味。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢穿过堆满铁架床的走廊，在一间焊着厚铁栏杆的配药室里见到了前铁路医院副院长梁诺夫医生，以及他的学生、流调员古丽娅。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 在靠墙的一张铁床上，用医用牛皮带死死绑着一个年轻女孩。她在高烧中昏睡了三天，几乎没有醒来，手腕被皮带磨得皮开肉绽。但此刻，她身上的高热正在消退，原本硬化如蛇皮的灰白角质正大片大片地从胸口和颈部剥落，露出底下粉红色的新生皮肤。她的呼吸虚弱而均匀。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 古丽娅医师眼睛通红，手里紧紧攥着三页薄薄的病历纸，纸角浸透了黄褐色的药水污渍。

    古丽娅 （把病历纸塞到阿列克谢眼前，手指由于激动而泛白） 第三十七例！阿廖沙，这是第三十七例完整记录！只要给他们水，熬过第四天的高热和抽搐，灰鳞就会开始剥落！这不是绝症，他们能自己挺过来！

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢接过那三页《回退临床手稿》。上面的每一行数据、每一个体温曲线，都用蓝黑墨水记录得清清楚楚。但在最后一页的末尾，盖着一个刺眼的红色长方形印章：【退回 · 结论含有动摇防线内容 · 不予归档】。

    梁诺夫医生 （坐在药箱上，用止血钳夹着棉球擦拭眼镜） 三天前，我把手稿交给了督察专员。他甚至没翻开第二页，直接当着我的面扔进了碎纸炉。他说：‘梁诺夫同志，布拉风号已经拉走了四千人，乌拉尔山线已经平了四千笔账。如果你的报告是真的，那四千个被销账的人算什么？’

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 老医生的手停在半空，眼眶凹陷。

    梁诺夫医生 他们不是不知道人能好起来。他们是不能让活着的人好起来。活着的人一退烧，他们手里的枪和单子就全成了杀人罪证。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 铁床上的女孩突然发出一声微弱的呻吟。阿列克谢猛地转过头。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 她艰难地睁开眼睛，瞳孔清澈透明。她看了看阿列克谢胸前的铁路调度工牌，又看向他大衣口袋里露出的妹妹照片，干裂的嘴唇吃力地开合：

    安娜 ……照片……0420……是拉娜护士吗？

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢像触电一样冲到铁床边，单膝跪地。

    阿列克谢 是我妹妹。你怎么会在疗养院？

    安娜 我是安娜，档案号 0419。清杀队来留观站押人时，我烧到休克，被当成死人丢在站台上。梁医生和古丽娅发现我还有气，把我带回疗养院。高烧时我怕伤人，才用皮带把自己绑在床栏上。

    阿列克谢 那你最后一次见拉娜时，她还醒着吗？

    安娜 昨晚装车前，我短暂醒过一次。拉娜让我再看一眼体温计：三十六度八。她手背的鳞都掉了，还隔着铁网把半片阿司匹林塞给我。她让我记住你在货运站，说你会来接她。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢把照片塞回口袋，几次都没对准袋口。档案上只剩个编号。现在他有个活人要接回来。

    English

    Old Infectious Disease Sanatorium, second basement level.

    This was once a tuberculosis isolation ward; white nitrate frost has bloomed across the dark, damp concrete. The air is heavy with the strange reek of strong carbolic acid and rotting tissue.

    Aleksei passes down a corridor heaped with iron-framed beds and finds Dr. Leonov, former deputy director of the railway hospital, together with his student, the epidemiologist Gulya, in a dispensary room behind thick welded iron bars.

    On an iron bed against the wall, a young girl is strapped down with medical leather belts. She has spent three days lost in a fevered sleep, barely waking; the straps have rubbed her wrists raw. But now her fever is breaking. Grey-white keratin, hardened like snakeskin, is sloughing from her chest and neck in great sheets, revealing pink new skin beneath. Her breathing is faint but even.

    Dr. Gulya's eyes are red-rimmed; she clutches three thin sheets of case notes in her hand, their corners soaked through with yellow-brown medicine stains.

    Case thirty-seven! Alyosha, the thirty-seventh complete record! Keep them hydrated and get them through the fever and convulsions on the fourth day; the Grey Scale starts to slough off! This isn't terminal. They can pull through on their own.

    Aleksei takes the three pages of the Clinical Regression Manuscript. Every line of data, every temperature curve, is set down plainly in blue-black ink. But at the foot of the last page is a glaring red rectangular stamp: [REJECTED · CONCLUSIONS CONTAIN MATERIAL THAT UNDERMINES THE LINE · NOT TO BE FILED].

    Three days ago I handed the manuscript to the inspector. He did not even open the second page — he threw it into the shredder before my eyes. He said: “Comrade Leonov, the armoured train Buran has already carried away four thousand people, and the Ural line has balanced four thousand accounts. If your report is true, what does that make those four thousand written-off souls?”

    The old doctor's hand hangs motionless in mid-air; his eyes are sunken in their sockets.

    It is not that they don't know people can get better. They cannot allow the living to get better. The moment a living person's fever breaks, the guns and the forms in their hands become evidence of murder.

    The girl on the iron bed lets out a faint moan. Aleksei turns sharply toward her.

    She opens her eyes with difficulty, her pupils clear. She looks at the railway dispatcher's badge on Aleksei's chest, then at the photograph of his sister peeking from his coat pocket. Her cracked lips move with effort:

    “...The photograph... 0420... is that Nurse Lana?”

    Aleksei rushes to the iron bed as though struck by lightning and drops to one knee.

    She's my sister. How did you get to the sanatorium?

    I'm Anna, file 0419. When the OSK came to take people from the observation ward, I went into shock from the fever and collapsed on the platform. They left me there for dead. Dr. Leonov and Gulya found I was still breathing and brought me back to the sanatorium. I was afraid I'd hurt someone in the fever, so I strapped myself to the bed rail.

    When you last saw Lana, was she still awake?

    Before they loaded her last night, I woke briefly. Lana asked me to check the thermometer once more: thirty-six point eight. The scales had fallen from the back of her hand. She slipped me half an aspirin through the wire. She told me to remember you worked at the freight yard and said you would come for her.

    Aleksei tries twice to put the photograph away before finding the pocket. The file held a number. Now he has someone alive to bring home.

    选项与回执
    1. 撬开铁笼，带她们走（消耗 1 份救援药品）

      你用撬棍崩开了生锈的笼锁，替她解开了嵌进肉里的皮带。安娜（0419）与古丽娅医师搀扶着跟上了你，铁笼门轴在极夜的风雪里发出一声长长的、如释重负的悲鸣。

      English

      Pry the cage. Bring them out. (-1 medical) Anna (0419) and Dr. Gulya join. The cage hinge screams in the snow.

回退手稿第二幕 / 旧传染病疗养院 · 地下室 / 战役对白 / 灰烬同行

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 老医生的手停在半空，眼眶凹陷。

    梁诺夫医生 他们不是不知道人能好起来。他们是不能让活着的人好起来。活着的人一退烧，他们手里的枪和单子就全成了杀人罪证。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 铁床上的女孩突然发出一声微弱的呻吟。阿列克谢猛地转过头。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 她艰难地睁开眼睛，瞳孔清澈透明。她看了看阿列克谢胸前的铁路调度工牌，又看向他大衣口袋里露出的妹妹照片，干裂的嘴唇吃力地开合：

    安娜 ……照片……0420……是拉娜护士吗？

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢像触电一样冲到铁床边，单膝跪地。

    阿列克谢 是我妹妹。你怎么会在疗养院？

    安娜 我是安娜，档案号 0419。清杀队来留观站押人时，我烧到休克，被当成死人丢在站台上。梁医生和古丽娅发现我还有气，把我带回疗养院。高烧时我怕伤人，才用皮带把自己绑在床栏上。

    阿列克谢 那你最后一次见拉娜时，她还醒着吗？

    安娜 昨晚装车前，我短暂醒过一次。拉娜让我再看一眼体温计：三十六度八。她手背的鳞都掉了，还隔着铁网把半片阿司匹林塞给我。她让我记住你在货运站，说你会来接她。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢把照片塞回口袋，几次都没对准袋口。档案上只剩个编号。现在他有个活人要接回来。

    English

    Old Infectious Disease Sanatorium, second basement level.

    This was once a tuberculosis isolation ward; white nitrate frost has bloomed across the dark, damp concrete. The air is heavy with the strange reek of strong carbolic acid and rotting tissue.

    Aleksei passes down a corridor heaped with iron-framed beds and finds Dr. Leonov, former deputy director of the railway hospital, together with his student, the epidemiologist Gulya, in a dispensary room behind thick welded iron bars.

    On an iron bed against the wall, a young girl is strapped down with medical leather belts. She has spent three days lost in a fevered sleep, barely waking; the straps have rubbed her wrists raw. But now her fever is breaking. Grey-white keratin, hardened like snakeskin, is sloughing from her chest and neck in great sheets, revealing pink new skin beneath. Her breathing is faint but even.

    Dr. Gulya's eyes are red-rimmed; she clutches three thin sheets of case notes in her hand, their corners soaked through with yellow-brown medicine stains.

    Case thirty-seven! Alyosha, the thirty-seventh complete record! Keep them hydrated and get them through the fever and convulsions on the fourth day; the Grey Scale starts to slough off! This isn't terminal. They can pull through on their own.

    Aleksei takes the three pages of the Clinical Regression Manuscript. Every line of data, every temperature curve, is set down plainly in blue-black ink. But at the foot of the last page is a glaring red rectangular stamp: [REJECTED · CONCLUSIONS CONTAIN MATERIAL THAT UNDERMINES THE LINE · NOT TO BE FILED].

    Three days ago I handed the manuscript to the inspector. He did not even open the second page — he threw it into the shredder before my eyes. He said: “Comrade Leonov, the armoured train Buran has already carried away four thousand people, and the Ural line has balanced four thousand accounts. If your report is true, what does that make those four thousand written-off souls?”

    The old doctor's hand hangs motionless in mid-air; his eyes are sunken in their sockets.

    It is not that they don't know people can get better. They cannot allow the living to get better. The moment a living person's fever breaks, the guns and the forms in their hands become evidence of murder.

    The girl on the iron bed lets out a faint moan. Aleksei turns sharply toward her.

    She opens her eyes with difficulty, her pupils clear. She looks at the railway dispatcher's badge on Aleksei's chest, then at the photograph of his sister peeking from his coat pocket. Her cracked lips move with effort:

    “...The photograph... 0420... is that Nurse Lana?”

    Aleksei rushes to the iron bed as though struck by lightning and drops to one knee.

    She's my sister. How did you get to the sanatorium?

    I'm Anna, file 0419. When the OSK came to take people from the observation ward, I went into shock from the fever and collapsed on the platform. They left me there for dead. Dr. Leonov and Gulya found I was still breathing and brought me back to the sanatorium. I was afraid I'd hurt someone in the fever, so I strapped myself to the bed rail.

    When you last saw Lana, was she still awake?

    Before they loaded her last night, I woke briefly. Lana asked me to check the thermometer once more: thirty-six point eight. The scales had fallen from the back of her hand. She slipped me half an aspirin through the wire. She told me to remember you worked at the freight yard and said you would come for her.

    Aleksei tries twice to put the photograph away before finding the pocket. The file held a number. Now he has someone alive to bring home.

    选项与回执
    1. 撬开铁笼，带她们走（消耗 1 份救援药品）

      你用撬棍崩开了生锈的笼锁，替她解开了嵌进肉里的皮带。安娜（0419）与古丽娅医师搀扶着跟上了你，铁笼门轴在极夜的风雪里发出一声长长的、如释重负的悲鸣。

      English

      Pry the cage. Bring them out. (-1 medical) Anna (0419) and Dr. Gulya join. The cage hinge screams in the snow.

黑市与老轨第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、药贩、伊万

查看地图

  1. 旁白 / 阿列克谢 午夜。废弃机车调车库。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 这里是诺沃斯克-17 最后的地下黑市。废弃机车库的铁轨间挤着帆布棚、补锅摊和换药的柜台；炉火把棚顶熏黑，几处灯还亮着，摊主敲打零件的声音一直没停。清杀队的巡逻车只在外围绕行——棚户巷窄，废车和岔道多，灯一照进去，反倒成了私兵的靶场。几名拿旧步枪和道钉枪的守卫看住出入口，狂暴者闯近时，他们就鸣枪把人赶回铁轨另一侧。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 这里谈不上富足，但比配给站更像一个还在运转的地方：有人修鞋、有人磨刀，也有人拿半袋煤换一把抗生素。被户籍簿和配给表一笔划掉的人，都挤在这片黑区里，靠交换、修补和彼此守夜撑下去。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 几个药贩子围在一台老式矿石收音机旁。短波 6.66 MHz 上反复传出黑市商贩的买卖暗号：【六六六……土豆换鳞粉……烙铁保平安……】那是摊主招揽生意的呼号，不是求救信号。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 在车厢暗处，一个假药贩正将一根烧得通红的火烙铁按在一个工人的手背上。焦糊的人肉臭味伴随着惨叫声散开，工人原本轻微起鳞的手背被硬生生烫成一片焦黑的烂肉。

    假药贩 （收走工人的手表，冷笑） 喊什么喊！烫成三级烧伤，清杀队的刮片枪就测不出反应！这就叫‘工业事故’，懂不懂？不然明早直接拉上铁壳号！

    旁白 阿列克谢挑开帘子，用撬棍敲响铁皮桶。烙铁掉进雪泥，腾起一缕白汽。药贩退到煤堆后面。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 机车驾驶室里，老轨伊万正坐在悬空的铁座上，就着一听发绿的腌酸黄瓜喝着烈性工业酒精。

    老轨伊万 （把一张浸透机油的路线纸拍在仪表盘上） 小子，内务部特勤连押的是一百七十六个退了烧的人。疗养所是写给家属看的。铁壳号真要去鹰嘴崖 3 号废矿。列车一进矿区，他们就封死洞口，把人和账一道抹掉。发车时刻我不知道，只有西站调度室那道盖红章的令上有。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢摊平路线纸。编组单上那行「销账点」忽然有了一个地名。他把指尖按在矿坑口，正好盖住那一小块黑墨。

    阿列克谢 你怎么混进编组站的？

    老轨伊万 （吐了口浓痰） 我在这条山线上开了二十五年。西站有张检修图，抄出来。车真停在鹰嘴崖，你得先知道从哪儿下到谷底。还有，市场东边那间旧信号检修室，我在 4.17 MHz 上听到过几秒短波回声。清杀队的监听组正在找这段频率，发报的人应该还在附近。

    English

    Midnight. The abandoned locomotive roundhouse.

    This is the last underground market in Novorsk-17. Canvas stalls, repair benches, and medicine counters crowd the tracks between abandoned locomotives. Stove smoke blackens the roofs; a few lamps still burn, and stallholders keep hammering at parts. OSK patrol trucks circle the perimeter but do not enter the narrow lanes and branching rows of derelict cars. Armed guards with old rifles and rail spikes watch the entrances, driving Rabid back across the tracks when they come near.

    There is no real abundance here, but the place feels more alive than the ration office: someone repairs boots, someone sharpens knives, someone trades half a sack of coal for antibiotics. People crossed out of the census and ration ledgers crowd into this black zone, surviving by barter, repairs, and keeping watch for one another.

    A few medicine sellers crowd around an old crystal radio. On shortwave 6.66 MHz, the black-market traders repeat their barter code: [Six-six-six... potatoes for scale-powder... the branding iron keeps you safe...]. It is a call for business, not a distress signal.

    In the dark of a boxcar, a fake-medicine peddler was pressing a red-hot branding iron against a worker's hand. The reek of scorched flesh spread with the man's screams; the back of his hand, where the scales had only just begun to show, was seared into a charred mass of raw meat.

    What are you yelling about? Burned that badly, their scraper won't register anything. That's an ‘industrial accident,’ understand? Otherwise, they'll put you straight on the Buran in the morning!

    Aleksei lifts the curtain and strikes a tin drum with his crowbar. The iron lands in the slush. A curl of steam rises, and the vendor backs behind the coal pile.

    In the locomotive cab, Old Ivan sat on a hanging iron seat, drinking potent industrial alcohol and chasing it with a tin of green pickles.

    Kid, the OSK special unit is escorting a hundred and seventy-six people whose fevers have broken. The sanatorium is what they tell the families. The Buran is really headed to the abandoned Pit No. 3 at Eagle's Crag. Once the train enters the mine, they will seal the entrance and erase the people along with the records. I don't know the departure time; only the red-stamped order in the West Station dispatch room has it.

    Aleksei spreads out the route sheet. The closure point on the consist has a name now. His fingertip covers the little black mark at the mouth of the pit.

    How did you get into the marshalling yard?

    I've driven this mountain line for twenty-five years. I copied a maintenance map from West Station. If the train stops at Eagle's Crag, you need to know how to get down to the valley. There's something else: I heard a few seconds of shortwave echo at 4.17 MHz from the old signal workshop east of the market. The OSK listening unit is tracking that frequency. Whoever sent it should still be nearby.

    选项与回执
    1. C1 公平交易：付 2 废钢买路线（中立）

      伊万收下废钢。油纸背面还多了一串短波坐标：6.66 MHz。那不是庄园路线，而是一处藏在市场里的信号源。

      English

      C1 Fair trade: pay 2 Scrap for the route Ivan takes the scrap. On the back of the oil paper is a shortwave coordinate: 6.66 MHz. It points to a hidden transmitter in the market.

    2. C3 倾囊相助：把抗生素分给这里冻伤的人（良知▲）

      看着你把救命的药塞进冻烂脚趾的苦人手里，伊万把一只烤热的厚帆布手套塞进你怀里。他还在油纸上圈出短波频率 6.66 MHz，告诉你去市场最深的电台棚找信号源。

      English

      C3 Give everything: hand out your antibiotics Old Ivan joins your crew and marks a 6.66 MHz signal source at the deepest market stall.

    3. C2 背叛：趁他引开哨兵，锁死铁门拿走磁卡（良知▼·卌+1）

      沉重的铁门被你从外头死死落了锁。门后先是一声难以置信的闷哼，紧接着是微声步枪短促得像踩碎冰碴似的点射声。你的背包被抢来的物资撑得满满当当，可你的灵魂却在日志背面多了一道洗不掉的血痕。

      English

      C2 Betray: lock the door and take the keycard The door shuts behind you. The shots are very short.

黑市与老轨第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 战役对白 / 失母／暂住线索

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、药贩、伊万

查看地图

  1. 旁白 / 阿列克谢 午夜。废弃机车调车库。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 这里是诺沃斯克-17 最后的地下黑市。废弃机车库的铁轨间挤着帆布棚、补锅摊和换药的柜台；炉火把棚顶熏黑，几处灯还亮着，摊主敲打零件的声音一直没停。清杀队的巡逻车只在外围绕行——棚户巷窄，废车和岔道多，灯一照进去，反倒成了私兵的靶场。几名拿旧步枪和道钉枪的守卫看住出入口，狂暴者闯近时，他们就鸣枪把人赶回铁轨另一侧。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 这里谈不上富足，但比配给站更像一个还在运转的地方：有人修鞋、有人磨刀，也有人拿半袋煤换一把抗生素。被户籍簿和配给表一笔划掉的人，都挤在这片黑区里，靠交换、修补和彼此守夜撑下去。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 几个药贩子围在一台老式矿石收音机旁。短波 6.66 MHz 上反复传出黑市商贩的买卖暗号：【六六六……土豆换鳞粉……烙铁保平安……】那是摊主招揽生意的呼号，不是求救信号。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 在车厢暗处，一个假药贩正将一根烧得通红的火烙铁按在一个工人的手背上。焦糊的人肉臭味伴随着惨叫声散开，工人原本轻微起鳞的手背被硬生生烫成一片焦黑的烂肉。

    假药贩 （收走工人的手表，冷笑） 喊什么喊！烫成三级烧伤，清杀队的刮片枪就测不出反应！这就叫‘工业事故’，懂不懂？不然明早直接拉上铁壳号！

    旁白 阿列克谢挑开帘子，用撬棍敲响铁皮桶。烙铁掉进雪泥，腾起一缕白汽。药贩退到煤堆后面。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 机车驾驶室里，老轨伊万正坐在悬空的铁座上，就着一听发绿的腌酸黄瓜喝着烈性工业酒精。

    老轨伊万 （把一张浸透机油的路线纸拍在仪表盘上） 小子，内务部特勤连押的是一百七十六个退了烧的人。疗养所是写给家属看的。铁壳号真要去鹰嘴崖 3 号废矿。列车一进矿区，他们就封死洞口，把人和账一道抹掉。发车时刻我不知道，只有西站调度室那道盖红章的令上有。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢摊平路线纸。编组单上那行「销账点」忽然有了一个地名。他把指尖按在矿坑口，正好盖住那一小块黑墨。

    阿列克谢 你怎么混进编组站的？

    老轨伊万 （吐了口浓痰） 我在这条山线上开了二十五年。西站有张检修图，抄出来。车真停在鹰嘴崖，你得先知道从哪儿下到谷底。还有，市场东边那间旧信号检修室，我在 4.17 MHz 上听到过几秒短波回声。清杀队的监听组正在找这段频率，发报的人应该还在附近。

    阿列克谢 母亲走后，602 室只剩那台停下来的机器。今晚我得找到一张床，哪怕只够躲过清杀队的搜查。

    旁白 市场最里面有间废信号室，门后能睡人。钥匙留在门边，先活着找到它。

    English

    Midnight. The abandoned locomotive roundhouse.

    This is the last underground market in Novorsk-17. Canvas stalls, repair benches, and medicine counters crowd the tracks between abandoned locomotives. Stove smoke blackens the roofs; a few lamps still burn, and stallholders keep hammering at parts. OSK patrol trucks circle the perimeter but do not enter the narrow lanes and branching rows of derelict cars. Armed guards with old rifles and rail spikes watch the entrances, driving Rabid back across the tracks when they come near.

    There is no real abundance here, but the place feels more alive than the ration office: someone repairs boots, someone sharpens knives, someone trades half a sack of coal for antibiotics. People crossed out of the census and ration ledgers crowd into this black zone, surviving by barter, repairs, and keeping watch for one another.

    A few medicine sellers crowd around an old crystal radio. On shortwave 6.66 MHz, the black-market traders repeat their barter code: [Six-six-six... potatoes for scale-powder... the branding iron keeps you safe...]. It is a call for business, not a distress signal.

    In the dark of a boxcar, a fake-medicine peddler was pressing a red-hot branding iron against a worker's hand. The reek of scorched flesh spread with the man's screams; the back of his hand, where the scales had only just begun to show, was seared into a charred mass of raw meat.

    What are you yelling about? Burned that badly, their scraper won't register anything. That's an ‘industrial accident,’ understand? Otherwise, they'll put you straight on the Buran in the morning!

    Aleksei lifts the curtain and strikes a tin drum with his crowbar. The iron lands in the slush. A curl of steam rises, and the vendor backs behind the coal pile.

    In the locomotive cab, Old Ivan sat on a hanging iron seat, drinking potent industrial alcohol and chasing it with a tin of green pickles.

    Kid, the OSK special unit is escorting a hundred and seventy-six people whose fevers have broken. The sanatorium is what they tell the families. The Buran is really headed to the abandoned Pit No. 3 at Eagle's Crag. Once the train enters the mine, they will seal the entrance and erase the people along with the records. I don't know the departure time; only the red-stamped order in the West Station dispatch room has it.

    Aleksei spreads out the route sheet. The closure point on the consist has a name now. His fingertip covers the little black mark at the mouth of the pit.

    How did you get into the marshalling yard?

    I've driven this mountain line for twenty-five years. I copied a maintenance map from West Station. If the train stops at Eagle's Crag, you need to know how to get down to the valley. There's something else: I heard a few seconds of shortwave echo at 4.17 MHz from the old signal workshop east of the market. The OSK listening unit is tracking that frequency. Whoever sent it should still be nearby.

    After Mother died, all that remained in Room 602 was the silent machine. Tonight I need a bed, even one that only hides me from OSK.

    There is an abandoned signal room at the back of the market. A key rests by the door; find it alive.

    选项与回执
    1. C1 公平交易：付 2 废钢买路线（中立）

      伊万收下废钢。油纸背面还多了一串短波坐标：6.66 MHz。那不是庄园路线，而是一处藏在市场里的信号源。

      English

      C1 Fair trade: pay 2 Scrap for the route Ivan takes the scrap. On the back of the oil paper is a shortwave coordinate: 6.66 MHz. It points to a hidden transmitter in the market.

    2. C3 倾囊相助：把抗生素分给这里冻伤的人（良知▲）

      看着你把救命的药塞进冻烂脚趾的苦人手里，伊万把一只烤热的厚帆布手套塞进你怀里。他还在油纸上圈出短波频率 6.66 MHz，告诉你去市场最深的电台棚找信号源。

      English

      C3 Give everything: hand out your antibiotics Old Ivan joins your crew and marks a 6.66 MHz signal source at the deepest market stall.

    3. C2 背叛：趁他引开哨兵，锁死铁门拿走磁卡（良知▼·卌+1）

      沉重的铁门被你从外头死死落了锁。门后先是一声难以置信的闷哼，紧接着是微声步枪短促得像踩碎冰碴似的点射声。你的背包被抢来的物资撑得满满当当，可你的灵魂却在日志背面多了一道洗不掉的血痕。

      English

      C2 Betray: lock the door and take the keycard The door shuts behind you. The shots are very short.

黑市与老轨第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 战役对白 / 失母／伊万同行

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、药贩、伊万

查看地图

    老轨伊万 市场最里面有间废信号室，门后能睡人。先活着找到它，别问是谁把钥匙留在那儿。

    English

失母后的黑市暂住第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢

查看地图

  1. 阿列克谢 废信号室的门在身后合上。炉子没烧起来，桌上只有一台还能收到微弱短波的旧电台。至少这里没有巡逻靴声。

    旁白 电台里断断续续传来一串调度呼号。阿列克谢把频率记进日志，没有听见熟悉的声音。

    阿列克谢 我会回来。妈妈的围巾还在床边，拉娜的事也还没完。先活过今晚。

    English

    The abandoned signal room door shuts behind him. The stove is cold; an old shortwave set on the table still catches a faint signal. At least there are no patrol boots here.

    A broken dispatch call sign comes over the radio. Aleksei writes down the frequency and hears no familiar voice.

    I'll come back. Mother's scarf is still by the bed. Lana's case is still open. First I need to get through tonight.

    选项与回执
    1. 锁好门，在短波声里歇一会儿

      黑市栖身处能暂时躲开清杀队。602 室仍保留为母亲的纪念地和物资据点。

      English

      Lock the door and rest to the shortwave The black-market refuge can hide you from OSK for now. Room 602 remains your memorial and supply base.

失母后的黑市暂住第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 战役对白 / 伊万同行

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、伊万

查看地图

  1. 阿列克谢 废信号室的门在身后合上。炉子没烧起来，桌上只有一台还能收到微弱短波的旧电台。至少这里没有巡逻靴声。

    老轨伊万 别把这儿当家，阿廖沙。就当一段临时站台。等你缓过来，再回 602 把该带的东西拿上。

    阿列克谢 我会回来。妈妈的围巾还在床边，拉娜的事也还没完。先活过今晚。

    English

    The abandoned signal room door shuts behind him. The stove is cold; an old shortwave set on the table still catches a faint signal. At least there are no patrol boots here.

    Don't call this home, Alyosha. Think of it as a temporary platform. When you can stand again, go back to 602 for what you need.

    I'll come back. Mother's scarf is still by the bed. Lana's case is still open. First I need to get through tonight.

    选项与回执
    1. 锁好门，在短波声里歇一会儿

      黑市栖身处能暂时躲开清杀队。602 室仍保留为母亲的纪念地和物资据点。

      English

      Lock the door and rest to the shortwave The black-market refuge can hide you from OSK for now. Room 602 remains your memorial and supply base.

4.17 MHz 与旧信号检修室第三幕 / 铁路黑市（废弃工人镇） / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、叶莲娜

查看地图

  1. 旁白 / 阿列克谢 市场地图东侧棚户巷标着一间旧铁路信号检修室。阿列克谢推开门时，收音机的 6.66 MHz 买卖暗号已经听不见了；桌上的短波机却刚从 4.17 MHz 切回静音。电池、手摇发电柄和压在旋钮下的庄园平面图说明，这个固定发射点已用了很久。内务部的监听组也在追这段频率，附近每多响几秒，搜查圈就会收紧一圈。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 门后的女人没有举枪。她先关掉发射机，再把监听耳机摘下来。她是茹科夫专员的女儿叶莲娜，也是这条秘密广播的架设者。她用低功率的 4.17 MHz 短波，断续向城外的医疗救援频道通报退烧者仍然活着；内务部正沿着这段频率测向搜查。她把发射点固定在这间旧信号检修室，也保留了一条从排污检修管进入松林庄园的路线。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 叶莲娜留着金色长发，穿着剪裁合身、面料厚实的冬季外套，领口的毛边和袖口都打理得干净。她看起来比外面的人体面许多，但脸上没有庄园里那种安稳。

    叶莲娜 （把频率表推到桌子中央） 我播了很多次。终于有人回话，我却先把发射机关了。我父亲把账本和第三节的记录锁在庄园里。排污管格栅松了，我带你进去。

    阿列克谢 你知道这会让你失去什么吗？

    叶莲娜 知道。先把门打开，出去以后再算。

    English

    The market map marks an old railway signal workshop in the eastern shanty lane. When Aleksei opens the door, the traders' 6.66 MHz code is gone; the shortwave set on the table has just gone silent from 4.17 MHz. Batteries, a hand crank, and a manor plan pressed under the dial show that this fixed transmission point has been in use for a long time. OSK's listening unit is tracking the frequency; every few seconds on air tightens the search around it.

    The woman behind the door does not raise a gun. She switches off the transmitter and removes her headphones. She is Yelena, Commissioner Zhukov's daughter, and the one who built the secret broadcast. In brief, low-power bursts on 4.17 MHz, she tells a medical rescue channel outside the city that those whose fevers broke are still alive. OSK is triangulating the frequency. She keeps the transmitter in this fixed workshop and has preserved a route into Pine Manor through the drainage duct.

    Yelena wore her fair hair long and a well-tailored winter coat of heavy cloth, the fur trim at the collar and cuffs kept clean. She looked far more presentable than the people outside, yet there was none of the manor's ease on her face.

    I've sent the message many times. When someone finally answered, I shut the transmitter off first. My father has the ledgers and Boxcar Three's records locked in the manor. The drainage grate is loose. I'll take you in.

    Do you know what this will cost you?

    I know. Open the door first - we'll settle it once we're outside.

    选项与回执
    1. 带叶莲娜同行，按她的路线进入松林庄园

      叶莲娜把频率表和排污路线交给你，背起自己的监听设备，跟你一起离开信号室。松林庄园的路线现在开放。

      English

      Take Yelena with you and follow her route into Pine Manor Yelena gives you the frequency sheet and drainage route, shoulders her listening gear, and leaves with you. Pine Manor is now unlocked.

    2. 只记下排污路线，不带叶莲娜同行

      你记下排污管路线，没有接过她的监听设备。叶莲娜留在信号室，松林庄园的入口已经找到。

      English

      Copy the drainage route but leave Yelena behind You copy the drainage route but leave her listening gear. Yelena stays behind; the manor entrance is found.

松林庄园第三幕 / 松林庄园 · 二号恒温库 / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、茹科夫、维克多

查看地图

  1. 旁白 / 阿列克谢 松林庄园。这里是铁路局干部特供区。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 与外面零下三十度的严寒截然相反，四米高压电网阻隔的高墙内，暖气片烧得滚烫，室内温度高达二十六度。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 阿列克谢穿过排污管潜入二号恒温特供库。刺眼的白炽灯下，码放着整齐划一的瑞士进口 409 批透析液、精制白面粉、整箱的新西兰黄油与带大理石花纹的冷冻牛排。

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 后勤处长维克多正指挥着四个穿白色防化服的工人，将一整推车临期的腹膜透析液和牛排推进熊熊燃烧的工业焚化炉。黑色油腻的焦烟顺着高耸的烟囱排向夜空，化作整座城市上空飘落的焦油黑雪。

    阿列克谢 （从阴影中踏出，手里的道钉撬棍直指维克多） 全城每天为了半茶匙碎麦子去告密，你们把救命的药往炉子里填？！

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 维克多吓得倒退撞翻了推车。阴影里，松林庄园特权区主管茹科夫专员端着茶杯慢慢走了出来。他穿着剪裁合身的毛呢制服，脸上没有凶恶，只有一种深入骨髓的疲惫与冷淡。

    茹科夫专员 （吹了吹茶水上的浮沫，示意工人继续往炉子里铲） 莫罗佐夫调度员。你以为我是个贪污犯？还是个以杀人为乐的疯子？

    旁白 / 阿列克谢 茹科夫拉开抽屉，掏出一本盖满公章的配给审计总账，扔在阿列克谢脚下。

    茹科夫专员 诺沃斯克-17 的主管道早就烂透了。主脑算得很清楚：煤炭储备只够撑三十天，药品只够供应六百人，而城里焊死在筒子楼里的活人有整整四万。如果不把三万人打成‘已注销’，如果不把他们送进矿坑，全城的蒸汽三天之内就会彻底耗尽。到时候，所有人——包括你、你妈、我和我的女儿，全都会冻死在床板上。三十箱药分给三百人，你信不信今晚半夜他们就能拿菜刀互相把门砸烂？……我不是恶人，莫罗佐夫。恶人早冻死了，留下来的是算账的。把门焊死，牺牲一部分人，剩下的至少能活到开春。

    阿列克谢 （握紧撬棍，声音像从冰缝里挤出来） 可第三节车厢里的人，他们的烧已经退了！他们是活人！

    茹科夫专员 （冷漠地看着他） 在电传机里，划掉的名字就是死人。如果他们是活人，那我们过去这一个月算什么？算刽子手？……莫罗佐夫，机器不能犯错。机器一犯错，整座城就会像雪崩一样塌掉。把枪放下，去地下室挑两箱牛排和一箱透析液带走，回你的 602 室去。就当这世上只有你母亲一个人。

    茹科夫专员 地下室还有几个人，名单上写的是零。桌上有一张配给凭条，也有能留下焚毁记录的底片。

    阿列克谢 门后还有人在敲。账本和配给都在眼前，我得先决定带走什么。

    English

    Pine Manor. This was the privileged compound of the railway bureau's cadres.

    In brutal contrast to the minus-thirty cold outside, inside the high walls behind a four-metre high-voltage electric fence the radiators ran scalding hot and the air reached twenty-six degrees.

    Aleksei slipped through the sewage duct into the No. 2 climate-controlled store. Beneath the glare of incandescent lamps stood neat rows of Swiss-imported Batch-409 dialysis fluid, refined white flour, crated New Zealand butter, and frozen steaks marbled with fat.

    Logistics Chief Viktor was directing four workers in white chemical-protection suits as they shovelled a whole cartload of near-expiry peritoneal dialysis fluid and steaks into a roaring industrial incinerator. Black, greasy smoke climbed the towering chimney into the night sky and turned into tar-black snow drifting down over the whole city.

    The whole city informs on its neighbours every day for half a teaspoon of cracked grain - and you're shovelling life-saving medicine into the furnace?!

    Viktor recoiled in fright and knocked over the cart. Out of the shadows came Commissioner Zhukov, the man who oversaw the privileged zone of Pine Manor, a teacup in his hand. He wore a well-tailored wool uniform, and there was no cruelty on his face - only a weariness and coldness that went to the bone.

    Dispatcher Morozov. Do you take me for an embezzler? Or a madman who kills for pleasure?

    Zhukov pulled out a drawer, took out a ration-audit master ledger stamped all over with official seals, and threw it down at Aleksei's feet.

    Novorsk-17's main pipeline rotted through long ago. The central brain worked it out precisely: the coal reserves would last thirty days, the medicine could supply six hundred people - and there were forty thousand living people sealed into the Khrushchyovka blocks. Unless thirty thousand of them were written off as 'deregistered', unless they were sent into the mines, the city's steam would be utterly exhausted within three days. When that happened, everyone - you, your mother, me, my daughter - would freeze to death on their bedboards. Divide thirty crates of medicine among three hundred people, and I promise you, by midnight tonight they'd be battering down each other's doors with kitchen knives. ... I'm not a villain, Morozov. The villains froze to death long ago. What's left are the ones who keep the accounts. Weld the doors shut, sacrifice a portion, and the rest may live at least until spring.

    But the people in the third carriage - their fevers have broken! They're alive!

    On the teletype, a name crossed out is a dead man. If they're alive, then what have we been for the past month? Executioners? ... Morozov, the machine cannot make mistakes. Once the machine makes a mistake, the whole city comes down like an avalanche. Put the gun down, go down to the cellar, pick out two crates of steak and one of dialysis fluid, and take them back to your Room 602. Just pretend your mother is the only person left in the world.

    There are several people in the cellar; the list calls them zero. On the desk are a ration slip and a negative that can preserve the burn record.

    Someone is still knocking behind the door. The ledger and ration are in front of me; I have to decide what to take first.

    选项与回执
    1. 留下台账照片，带走 409 批透析液

      你把相机握紧。先留下能对上账本的证据，再带透析液离开。

      English

      Keep the ledger photograph and take Batch-409 fluid. You hold on to the camera. First preserve evidence that can be checked against the ledger, then take the fluid out.

    2. 把老谢苗的藏身点交给茹科夫，换取配给

      你接过一张空白配给凭条。老谢苗的藏身处还没有写上去。

      English

      Give Zhukov old Semyon's hiding place in exchange for a ration allocation. You take a blank ration slip. Old Semyon's hiding place is not written on it yet.

    3. 趁对峙打开地下室，让工人先走

      地下室的敲门声没有停。你记下了门禁锁盒的位置。

      English

      Open the cellar during the confrontation and let the workers leave first. The cellar knocking continues. You note the position of the access lock box.

    叶莲娜 通行证磕在杯托上，茶水漫过杯沿。「刚才那推车 409 批透析液进炉子，你闻见了吗？外头有人为半勺麦糊告密。你把药烧了，让我在这二十六度的屋子里喝茶。」

    茹科夫专员 茹科夫把杯子顿在桌上，杯沿裂了。「你以为这二十六度是怎么来的？门外那三万人进来，先拆暖气，再来扯你身上这件大衣。我划掉那些名字，就是为了让你还有命站在这里。」

    叶莲娜 叶莲娜把他的笔尖按向空白的一行。「门是我开的，水是我递的，6.66 MHz 的电台也是我架的。你说划掉的名字就是死人。好，写叶莲娜。盖你的红章，叫他们把我也装进第三节。」

    叶莲娜 笔尖悬着，墨在纸上洇开。茹科夫的手往铃边挪了一寸，又停下。叶莲娜松开手：「阿列克谢，趁现在。」

    The pass hits the saucer. Tea spills over the rim. “Did you smell the Batch-409 fluid when it went into the furnace? Outside, people inform on each other for half a spoonful of mash. You burned the medicine so I could drink tea in this room at twenty-six degrees.”

    Zhukov puts the cup down hard enough to crack the rim. “Where do you think those twenty-six degrees came from? If those thirty thousand get in, first they’ll tear out the radiators. Then they’ll tear that coat off your back. I crossed out those names to keep you standing here.”

    Yelena guides his pen to an empty line. “I opened the door. I brought them water. I set up the 6.66 MHz station. You said a crossed-out name was a dead person. Go on. Write Yelena. Stamp it red and have them put me in Boxcar Three.”

    The nib hangs there. Ink spreads into the paper. Zhukov’s hand moves an inch toward the bell, then stops. Yelena lets go. “Aleksei. Now.”

西站发车令第四幕 / 西站调车场 / 战役对白

出场或被记录人物　阿列克谢、随行伙伴

查看地图

  1. 旁白 西站调度室外的防寒铁皮板上，孤零零地钉着一张还没来得及撕毁的绝密发车令，上面内务部的红章新鲜得发黏，像一滩刚刚凝固的血：

    旁白 【特别运输指令 · 严禁悬挂车次牌 · 发车时刻：23:00 · 行车区间：鹰嘴崖死线 · 第三节车厢核载：176 人 · 死令：实毁数与账面数必须绝对闭合】

    旁白 你拍下那个鲜红的印鉴。报废守车的工具箱底躺着一支拆开的旧莫辛纳甘。枪托裂了，枪机抹着黄油。你把它装好；枪膛里还剩三发。

    English

    A dispatch order is still pinned to the board outside the control room, the red stamp still tacky.

    [SPECIAL RUN · NO TRAIN NUMBER · 23:00 · EAGLE'S CRAG LINE · BOXCAR 3 · 176 PERSONS · PHYSICAL COUNT MUST EQUAL LEDGER]

    You photograph the fresh red stamp. At the bottom of the dead brake van's toolbox lies a disassembled Mosin–Nagant. The stock is split, and the bolt is greased. You fit it together; three rounds remain.

    选项与回执
    1. 带走莫辛纳甘，抄下车次时刻

      红章下面是 23:00。你把时刻抄在袖口，把莫辛纳甘背上肩。鹰嘴崖的路已经找到了。

      English

      Take the Mosin–Nagant and copy the timetable Beneath the red stamp: 23:00. You copy the time onto your cuff and sling the Mosin–Nagant over your shoulder. The road to Eagle's Crag is marked.

西站发车令第四幕 / 西站调车场 / 战役对白 / 灰烬（灰狼犬）／常态

    旁白 灰烬把湿冷的鼻尖抵在你靴边，低低呜咽。它抬头望着调车场入口。

    A dispatch order is still pinned to the board outside the control room, the red stamp still tacky.

    You photograph the fresh red stamp. At the bottom of the dead brake van's toolbox lies a disassembled Mosin–Nagant. The stock is split, and the bolt is greased. You fit it together; three rounds remain.

    Ash presses his cold, wet nose against your boot and whines. He looks toward the yard entrance.

    1. 带走莫辛纳甘，抄下车次时刻
